{"parentUuid":"a7ab1459-c118-444b-90c7-66492d930866","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"103cde84-18ec-4036-bbbf-c8f26cc8bae4","agentId":"a1f765d637aca55e2","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012RaoezgnGAdVKX2f7Kwkuh","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// COST CALCULATION MODULE\n2\t// Handles pricing calculations for STT providers and LLM post-processing\n3\t\n4\timport { roundUpToTenth } from './utils';\n5\t\n6\t// =============================================================================\n7\t// PRICING CONSTANTS\n8\t// =============================================================================\n9\t\n10\t// ElevenLabs Scribe v2 Pricing (USD)\n11\tconst ELEVENLABS_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.00983;\n12\tconst ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE = 0.20;   // +20% on base when keyterm prompting is used (scribe_v2)\n13\t\n14\t// Deepgram Nova-3 Pricing (USD)\n15\t// Base $0.0043 + features $0.0012\n16\tconst DEEPGRAM_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.0055;\n17\t\n18\t// Groq Whisper Pricing (USD) — per-model: turbo is ~2.8x cheaper than large-v3.\n19\t// https://groq.com/pricing — whisper-large-v3 $0.111/hr, turbo $0.04/hr.\n20\tconst GROQ_WHISPER_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR = 0.111; // $0.111/hour (whisper-large-v3)\n21\tconst GROQ_WHISPER_TURBO_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR = 0.04; // $0.04/hour (whisper-large-v3-turbo)\n22\tconst GROQ_WHISPER_MIN_BILLABLE_SECONDS = 10;\n23\t\n24\t// xAI Grok STT Pricing (USD)\n25\tconst XAI_STT_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR = 0.10;\n26\t\n27\t// Microsoft MAI-Transcribe 1.5 (Azure Speech) Pricing (USD)\n28\tconst AZURE_MAI_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.006;\n29\t\n30\t// Google Cloud Speech-to-Text V2 Chirp 3 Pricing (USD)\n31\tconst GOOGLE_CHIRP_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.016;\n32\t\n33\t// ── New HyperWhisper Cloud STT proxy providers (per-model) ──────────────────\n34\t// Each provider exposes one or more models with distinct pricing. Token-billed\n35\t// providers (OpenAI gpt-4o-*, Gemini) bill from the response `usage` object so\n36\t// the charge is exact; duration-billed providers bill from returned audio\n37\t// seconds. See shared-app-classification/cloud-stt-catalog.json for the\n38\t// client-facing credits/min surface.\n39\t\n40\t// OpenAI — whisper-1 is duration-billed; gpt-4o-* are token-billed.\n41\tconst OPENAI_WHISPER1_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.006;\n42\tconst OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 2.50 / 1_000_000;\n43\tconst OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_OUTPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 10.00 / 1_000_000;\n44\tconst OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.25 / 1_000_000;\n45\tconst OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_OUTPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 5.00 / 1_000_000;\n46\t// Per-minute floors used only when the token `usage` object is missing/changed,\n47\t// so a gpt-4o transcription never bills $0 (fail-closed).\n48\tconst OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_FLOOR_PER_MINUTE = 0.006;\n49\tconst OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_FLOOR_PER_MINUTE = 0.003;\n50\t\n51\t// Gemini — audio billed at 32 tokens/sec; cost read from usageMetadata. Input\n52\t// is multi-modal: TEXT (the instruction+vocab prompt) and AUDIO bill at\n53\t// DIFFERENT per-token rates on Flash models, so both must be tracked. Pro models\n54\t// (2.5-pro, 3.1-pro) bill every input modality at one rate but switch to a\n55\t// higher long-context tier above 200k prompt tokens. Rates: Standard paid tier,\n56\t// per-1M ÷ 1e6. Ref: https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/pricing\n57\tinterface GeminiRate {\n58\t  textInputPerToken: number;\n59\t  audioInputPerToken: number;\n60\t  outputPerToken: number;\n61\t  // Override rates for prompts above GEMINI_LONG_CONTEXT_THRESHOLD tokens.\n62\t  // Present only for models with tiered (Pro) pricing; flat models omit it.\n63\t  longContext?: { textInputPerToken: number; audioInputPerToken: number; outputPerToken: number };\n64\t}\n65\tconst GEMINI_LONG_CONTEXT_THRESHOLD = 200_000;\n66\tconst M = 1_000_000;\n67\tconst GEMINI_RATES: Record<string, GeminiRate> = {\n68\t  'gemini-2.5-flash': { textInputPerToken: 0.30 / M, audioInputPerToken: 1.00 / M, outputPerToken: 2.50 / M },\n69\t  'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': { textInputPerToken: 0.10 / M, audioInputPerToken: 0.30 / M, outputPerToken: 0.40 / M },\n70\t  'gemini-2.5-pro': {\n71\t    textInputPerToken: 1.25 / M, audioInputPerToken: 1.25 / M, outputPerToken: 10.00 / M,\n72\t    longContext: { textInputPerToken: 2.50 / M, audioInputPerToken: 2.50 / M, outputPerToken: 15.00 / M },\n73\t  },\n74\t  'gemini-3-flash-preview': { textInputPerToken: 0.50 / M, audioInputPerToken: 1.00 / M, outputPerToken: 3.00 / M },\n75\t  'gemini-3.1-pro-preview': {\n76\t    textInputPerToken: 2.00 / M, audioInputPerToken: 2.00 / M, outputPerToken: 12.00 / M,\n77\t    longContext: { textInputPerToken: 4.00 / M, audioInputPerToken: 4.00 / M, outputPerToken: 18.00 / M },\n78\t  },\n79\t};\n80\tconst GEMINI_FALLBACK_RATE = GEMINI_RATES['gemini-2.5-flash'];\n81\t\n82\t// AssemblyAI — duration-billed; medical is a +$0.15/hr add-on, not a model.\n83\tconst ASSEMBLYAI_UNIVERSAL2_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.15 / 60;       // $0.0025/min\n84\tconst ASSEMBLYAI_UNIVERSAL3_PRO_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.21 / 60;   // $0.0035/min\n85\tconst ASSEMBLYAI_MEDICAL_ADDON_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.15 / 60;    // +$0.0025/min\n86\tconst ASSEMBLYAI_KEYTERMS_ADDON_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.05 / 60;   // +~$0.05/hr (universal-3-pro only)\n87\t// AssemblyAI's separate sync product (<=120s clips, single blocking request)\n88\t// always runs universal-3-5-pro, a model the async tiers above don't cover —\n89\t// billed at its own published rate, \"the same rate as Universal-3.5 Pro\n90\t// Realtime\". No medical/keyterms add-ons: the sync API doesn't support them.\n91\t// Exported as the single source of truth for this rate: stt-models.ts's\n92\t// preflight reservation figure (`ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE`)\n93\t// imports it rather than hardcoding a second copy of the same literal, so the\n94\t// amount actually reserved can never silently drift from what's actually billed.\n95\texport const ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.45 / 60;             // $0.0075/min\n96\t\n97\t// Mistral Voxtral — per-audio-minute billing.\n98\tconst MISTRAL_VOXTRAL_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.003;\n99\t\n100\t// Soniox — token-billed. The ≈ $0.10/hr async equivalence covers input-AUDIO +\n101\t// OUTPUT-text tokens for typical speech; custom-context INPUT-text tokens are\n102\t// charged ON TOP at $3.50/1M (async). Soniox bills ~0.3 tokens per character.\n103\t// Ref: https://soniox.com/pricing\n104\tconst SONIOX_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.10 / 60;                       // ≈ $0.001667/min\n105\tconst SONIOX_INPUT_TEXT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 3.50 / 1_000_000;            // async context tokens\n106\tconst SONIOX_TOKENS_PER_CHAR = 0.3;\n107\texport function estimateSonioxContextTokens(contextText: string | undefined): number {\n108\t  if (!contextText) return 0;\n109\t  return Math.ceil(contextText.length * SONIOX_TOKENS_PER_CHAR);\n110\t}\n111\t\n112\t// Anthropic Claude Haiku 4.5 Pricing (USD)\n113\tconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.00 / 1_000_000;\n114\tconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 5.00 / 1_000_000;\n115\t// Prompt caching: writes bill at 1.25x input, reads at 0.10x input (5-minute TTL).\n116\tconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_WRITE_COST_PER_TOKEN = ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN * 1.25;\n117\tconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_READ_COST_PER_TOKEN = ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN * 0.10;\n118\t\n119\t// Cerebras GPT-OSS-120B Pricing (USD)\n120\tconst CEREBRAS_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.35 / 1_000_000;\n121\tconst CEREBRAS_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.75 / 1_000_000;\n122\t\n123\t// Groq GPT-OSS-120B Pricing (USD)\n124\tconst GROQ_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.15 / 1_000_000;\n125\tconst GROQ_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.60 / 1_000_000;\n126\t\n127\t// xAI Grok 4.1 Fast Pricing (USD)\n128\tconst XAI_GROK_FAST_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.25 / 1_000_000;\n129\tconst XAI_GROK_FAST_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 2.50 / 1_000_000;\n130\t\n131\t// ── HyperWhisper Cloud post-processing LLM providers (per-model) ────────────\n132\t// Each provider exposes one or more chat models with distinct input/output\n133\t// token pricing. These MUST match shared-app-classification/cloud-pp-catalog.json\n134\t// (the display source of truth) — they are the ACTUAL billing constants. Rates\n135\t// are USD per-1M ÷ 1e6. Re-confirmed against live provider pricing 2026-06-19.\n136\tinterface LLMChatRate {\n137\t  promptPerToken: number;\n138\t  completionPerToken: number;\n139\t}\n140\t\n141\t// OpenAI GPT-5 family (chat/completions). https://platform.openai.com/docs/pricing\n142\tconst OPENAI_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n143\t  'gpt-5-mini': { promptPerToken: 0.25 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 2.00 / 1_000_000 },\n144\t  'gpt-5-nano': { promptPerToken: 0.05 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.40 / 1_000_000 },\n145\t};\n146\tconst OPENAI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'gpt-5-mini';\n147\t\n148\t// Google Gemini (OpenAI-compatible endpoint). https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/pricing\n149\tconst GEMINI_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n150\t  'gemini-2.5-flash': { promptPerToken: 0.30 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 2.50 / 1_000_000 },\n151\t  'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': { promptPerToken: 0.10 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.40 / 1_000_000 },\n152\t};\n153\tconst GEMINI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'gemini-2.5-flash';\n154\t\n155\t// Mistral (chat/completions). https://mistral.ai/pricing + docs.mistral.ai\n156\t// `mistral-small-latest` resolves to Mistral Small 4: the model-specific docs\n157\t// list $0.15/$0.60, which conflicts with the lower pricing-tile figure — we\n158\t// bill at the higher (model-docs) rate, the billing-safe choice. `mistral-nemo`\n159\t// is not a valid chat id; the canonical API id is `open-mistral-nemo`.\n160\t// Re-confirm both against live provider pages before secrets go live.\n161\tconst MISTRAL_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n162\t  'mistral-small-latest': { promptPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.60 / 1_000_000 },\n163\t  'open-mistral-nemo': { promptPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000 },\n164\t};\n165\tconst MISTRAL_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'mistral-small-latest';\n166\t\n167\t// Credit model: 1 credit = $0.001\n168\tconst USD_PER_CREDIT = 0.001;\n169\t\n170\t// =============================================================================\n171\t// TYPES\n172\t// =============================================================================\n173\t\n174\texport interface GroqUsage {\n175\t  prompt_tokens: number;\n176\t  completion_tokens: number;\n177\t  total_tokens: number;\n178\t}\n179\t\n180\t// =============================================================================\n181\t// STT COSTS\n182\t// =============================================================================\n183\t\n184\texport function computeElevenLabsTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number, keyterms: boolean = false): number {\n185\t  const durationMinutes = durationSeconds / 60;\n186\t  const base = durationMinutes * ELEVENLABS_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n187\t  // ElevenLabs keyterm prompting (scribe_v2) carries a documented +20% surcharge\n188\t  // on the base transcription cost.\n189\t  const raw = keyterms ? base * (1 + ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE) : base;\n190\t  return roundUsd(raw);\n191\t}\n192\t\n193\texport function computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n194\t  const durationMinutes = durationSeconds / 60;\n195\t  const raw = durationMinutes * DEEPGRAM_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n196\t  return roundUsd(raw);\n197\t}\n198\t\n199\texport function computeGroqTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number, model?: string): number {\n200\t  const billableSeconds = Math.max(durationSeconds, GROQ_WHISPER_MIN_BILLABLE_SECONDS);\n201\t  // whisper-large-v3 bills at the higher rate; everything else (including the\n202\t  // default whisper-large-v3-turbo) bills at the turbo rate (~2.8x cheaper).\n203\t  const costPerHour = model === 'whisper-large-v3'\n204\t    ? GROQ_WHISPER_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR\n205\t    : GROQ_WHISPER_TURBO_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR;\n206\t  const raw = (billableSeconds / 3600) * costPerHour;\n207\t  return roundUsd(raw);\n208\t}\n209\t\n210\texport function computeXaiTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n211\t  const raw = (durationSeconds / 3600) * XAI_STT_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR;\n212\t  return roundUsd(raw);\n213\t}\n214\t\n215\texport function computeAzureMaiTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n216\t  const durationMinutes = durationSeconds / 60;\n217\t  const raw = durationMinutes * AZURE_MAI_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n218\t  return roundUsd(raw);\n219\t}\n220\t\n221\texport function computeGoogleChirpTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n222\t  const durationMinutes = durationSeconds / 60;\n223\t  const raw = durationMinutes * GOOGLE_CHIRP_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n224\t  return roundUsd(raw);\n225\t}\n226\t\n227\t// ── New cloud STT proxy providers ───────────────────────────────────────────\n228\t\n229\texport interface OpenAITranscriptionUsage {\n230\t  durationSeconds: number;\n231\t  inputTokens?: number;\n232\t  outputTokens?: number;\n233\t}\n234\t\n235\t/**\n236\t * OpenAI: `whisper-1` bills on audio duration; the `gpt-4o-*` transcribe models\n237\t * bill on tokens from the response `usage` object. The gpt-4o `prompt` text\n238\t * input tokens are billed at the (higher) audio-input rate — they're a handful\n239\t * of tokens, so erring high there is both simpler and conservative.\n240\t */\n241\texport function computeOpenAITranscriptionCost(model: string, usage: OpenAITranscriptionUsage): number {\n242\t  if (model === 'whisper-1') {\n243\t    return roundUsd((usage.durationSeconds / 60) * OPENAI_WHISPER1_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE);\n244\t  }\n245\t\n246\t  const inputTokens = Math.max(0, usage.inputTokens ?? 0);\n247\t  const outputTokens = Math.max(0, usage.outputTokens ?? 0);\n248\t  const isMini = model === 'gpt-4o-mini-transcribe';\n249\t\n250\t  const tokenCost = isMini\n251\t    ? inputTokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN\n252\t      + outputTokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_OUTPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN\n253\t    : inputTokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN\n254\t      + outputTokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_OUTPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n255\t\n256\t  if (tokenCost > 0) {\n257\t    return roundUsd(tokenCost);\n258\t  }\n259\t\n260\t  // Missing/changed `usage` schema would otherwise bill $0 — fall back to a\n261\t  // duration-based estimate so token-billed models fail closed, mirroring the\n262\t  // LLM `estimateUsageFromChars` safety net.\n263\t  const floorPerMinute = isMini\n264\t    ? OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_FLOOR_PER_MINUTE\n265\t    : OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_FLOOR_PER_MINUTE;\n266\t  return roundUsd((usage.durationSeconds / 60) * floorPerMinute);\n267\t}\n268\t\n269\texport interface GeminiTranscriptionUsage {\n270\t  audioInputTokens: number;\n271\t  /**\n272\t   * Non-audio prompt-input tokens (the instruction + vocabulary text we send).\n273\t   * Billed at the model's text-input rate — NOT the audio rate. The adapter\n274\t   * derives this as promptTokenCount − audioInputTokens so audio + text always\n275\t   * sums to the documented input total (no double-count, no unbilled remainder).\n276\t   */\n277\t  textInputTokens?: number;\n278\t  outputTokens: number;\n279\t  /** Duration estimate (from payload size) used only if usageMetadata is absent. */\n280\t  fallbackDurationSeconds?: number;\n281\t}\n282\t\n283\t// Gemini represents 1 minute of audio as 1,920 input tokens (32 tokens/sec).\n284\tconst GEMINI_AUDIO_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE = 1_920;\n285\t\n286\t/**\n287\t * Gemini bills audio-input + text-input + output tokens from `usageMetadata`.\n288\t * Text and audio input have different per-token rates on Flash models, so both\n289\t * are billed at their own rate. Pro models switch to a higher long-context tier\n290\t * once the prompt (audio + text input) exceeds 200k tokens. Output tokens\n291\t * include any thinking tokens (only 2.5-flash/flash-lite can zero thinking;\n292\t * Pro/3.x always charge some). If usageMetadata is missing we fall back to a\n293\t * duration-based estimate so a transcription never bills $0 (fail-closed).\n294\t */\n295\texport function computeGeminiTranscriptionCost(model: string, usage: GeminiTranscriptionUsage): number {\n296\t  const rateDef = GEMINI_RATES[model] ?? GEMINI_FALLBACK_RATE;\n297\t  const audioTokens = Math.max(0, usage.audioInputTokens);\n298\t  const textTokens = Math.max(0, usage.textInputTokens ?? 0);\n299\t  const outputTokens = Math.max(0, usage.outputTokens);\n300\t\n301\t  // Long-context tier is keyed on the total prompt (input) token count.\n302\t  const promptTokens = audioTokens + textTokens;\n303\t  const rate = (rateDef.longContext && promptTokens > GEMINI_LONG_CONTEXT_THRESHOLD)\n304\t    ? rateDef.longContext\n305\t    : rateDef;\n306\t\n307\t  const tokenCost = audioTokens * rate.audioInputPerToken\n308\t    + textTokens * rate.textInputPerToken\n309\t    + outputTokens * rate.outputPerToken;\n310\t\n311\t  if (tokenCost > 0) {\n312\t    return roundUsd(tokenCost);\n313\t  }\n314\t\n315\t  const seconds = Math.max(0, usage.fallbackDurationSeconds ?? 0);\n316\t  return roundUsd((seconds / 60) * GEMINI_AUDIO_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE * rateDef.audioInputPerToken);\n317\t}\n318\t\n319\t// ~4 chars/token — matches the Gemini adapter's prompt-text estimate.\n320\tconst RESERVATION_CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 4;\n321\t\n322\t/**\n323\t * Conservative USD for the prompt-text INPUT tokens a TOKEN-BILLED provider will\n324\t * charge for `initial_prompt` (Gemini, all models; OpenAI gpt-4o-transcribe /\n325\t * gpt-4o-mini-transcribe). Used ONLY by the preflight reservation so a large\n326\t * vocabulary prompt on a short clip can't be deducted beyond what was reserved.\n327\t * Duration-billed providers (whisper-1, deepgram/groq/grok/elevenlabs/azure/\n328\t * soniox/mistral) don't bill prompt tokens → 0; the ElevenLabs /\n329\t * AssemblyAI keyterm add-ons are reserved separately via the per-minute surcharge.\n330\t */\n331\texport function estimatePromptInputReservationUsd(\n332\t  provider: string,\n333\t  model: string | undefined,\n334\t  initialPrompt: string | undefined,\n335\t): number {\n336\t  if (!initialPrompt) return 0;\n337\t  const tokens = Math.ceil(initialPrompt.length / RESERVATION_CHARS_PER_TOKEN);\n338\t  if (provider === 'gemini') {\n339\t    const rate = GEMINI_RATES[model ?? ''] ?? GEMINI_FALLBACK_RATE;\n340\t    return tokens * rate.textInputPerToken;\n341\t  }\n342\t  if (provider === 'openai') {\n343\t    // whisper-1 is duration-billed — no prompt-token charge. Default + the\n344\t    // explicit gpt-4o-transcribe use the (more expensive) transcribe input rate.\n345\t    if (model === 'whisper-1') return 0;\n346\t    if (model === 'gpt-4o-mini-transcribe') return tokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n347\t    return tokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n348\t  }\n349\t  if (provider === 'soniox') {\n350\t    // Soniox charges the custom-context terms as async input-text tokens on top\n351\t    // of the audio/output blend. Its tokenizer (~0.3 tok/char) differs from the\n352\t    // 4-chars/token heuristic above, so use the Soniox-specific estimate.\n353\t    return estimateSonioxContextTokens(initialPrompt) * SONIOX_INPUT_TEXT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n354\t  }\n355\t  return 0;\n356\t}\n357\t\n358\t/**\n359\t * AssemblyAI duration-billed; `medical` layers the +$0.15/hr add-on on top of\n360\t * the chosen base model (universal-2 or universal-3-pro). `keyterms` layers the\n361\t * ~$0.05/hr keyterms-prompt add-on, but ONLY for universal-3-pro — universal-2\n362\t * keyterms are free/beta and must not be charged.\n363\t */\n364\texport function computeAssemblyAITranscriptionCost(\n365\t  durationSeconds: number,\n366\t  model: string,\n367\t  medical: boolean = false,\n368\t  keyterms: boolean = false,\n369\t): number {\n370\t  const basePerMinute = model === 'universal-3-pro'\n371\t    ? ASSEMBLYAI_UNIVERSAL3_PRO_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE\n372\t    : ASSEMBLYAI_UNIVERSAL2_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n373\t  const keytermsPerMinute = (keyterms && model === 'universal-3-pro')\n374\t    ? ASSEMBLYAI_KEYTERMS_ADDON_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE\n375\t    : 0;\n376\t  const perMinute = basePerMinute\n377\t    + (medical ? ASSEMBLYAI_MEDICAL_ADDON_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE : 0)\n378\t    + keytermsPerMinute;\n379\t  return roundUsd((durationSeconds / 60) * perMinute);\n380\t}\n381\t\n382\texport function computeAssemblyAISyncTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n383\t  return roundUsd((durationSeconds / 60) * ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE);\n384\t}\n385\t\n386\texport function computeMistralTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n387\t  return roundUsd((durationSeconds / 60) * MISTRAL_VOXTRAL_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE);\n388\t}\n389\t\n390\texport function computeSonioxTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number, contextTextTokens: number = 0): number {\n391\t  const audioAndOutput = (durationSeconds / 60) * SONIOX_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n392\t  const contextCost = Math.max(0, contextTextTokens) * SONIOX_INPUT_TEXT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n393\t  return roundUsd(audioAndOutput + contextCost);\n394\t}\n395\t\n396\t// =============================================================================\n397\t// LLM COSTS\n398\t// =============================================================================\n399\t\n400\texport function computeAnthropicCost(\n401\t  inputTokens: number,\n402\t  outputTokens: number,\n403\t  cacheCreationTokens: number = 0,\n404\t  cacheReadTokens: number = 0,\n405\t): number {\n406\t  // `inputTokens` is the uncached input delta; cache buckets are billed separately.\n407\t  const promptCost = inputTokens * ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n408\t  const completionCost = outputTokens * ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n409\t  const cacheWriteCost = cacheCreationTokens * ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_WRITE_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n410\t  const cacheReadCost = cacheReadTokens * ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_READ_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n411\t  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost + cacheWriteCost + cacheReadCost);\n412\t}\n413\t\n414\texport function computeCerebrasChatCost(usage: GroqUsage): number {\n415\t  const promptCost = usage.prompt_tokens * CEREBRAS_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n416\t  const completionCost = usage.completion_tokens * CEREBRAS_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n417\t  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost);\n418\t}\n419\t\n420\texport function computeGroqChatCost(usage: GroqUsage): number {\n421\t  const promptCost = usage.prompt_tokens * GROQ_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n422\t  const completionCost = usage.completion_tokens * GROQ_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n423\t  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost);\n424\t}\n425\t\n426\texport function computeXaiGrokFastChatCost(usage: GroqUsage): number {\n427\t  const promptCost = usage.prompt_tokens * XAI_GROK_FAST_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n428\t  const completionCost = usage.completion_tokens * XAI_GROK_FAST_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n429\t  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost);\n430\t}\n431\t\n432\t// Per-model chat cost for the OpenAI-compatible PP providers. Unknown models\n433\t// fall back to the provider default rate so a catalog/header drift never bills\n434\t// $0 (fail-closed, matching the duration-based STT floors above).\n435\tfunction computeChatCost(rates: Record<string, LLMChatRate>, defaultModel: string, model: string, usage: GroqUsage): number {\n436\t  const rate = rates[model] ?? rates[defaultModel];\n437\t  const promptCost = usage.prompt_tokens * rate.promptPerToken;\n438\t  const completionCost = usage.completion_tokens * rate.completionPerToken;\n439\t  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost);\n440\t}\n441\t\n442\texport function computeOpenAIChatCost(model: string, usage: GroqUsage): number {\n443\t  return computeChatCost(OPENAI_CHAT_RATES, OPENAI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL, model, usage);\n444\t}\n445\t\n446\texport function computeGeminiChatCost(model: string, usage: GroqUsage): number {\n447\t  return computeChatCost(GEMINI_CHAT_RATES, GEMINI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL, model, usage);\n448\t}\n449\t\n450\texport function computeMistralChatCost(model: string, usage: GroqUsage): number {\n451\t  return computeChatCost(MISTRAL_CHAT_RATES, MISTRAL_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL, model, usage);\n452\t}\n453\t\n454\t// Fallback token estimate (~4 chars/token) for when a provider response omits\n455\t// or changes the usage schema. Ensures vendor schema drift fails closed\n456\t// (billed via estimate) instead of open (costUsd = 0).\n457\tconst FALLBACK_CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 4;\n458\t\n459\texport function estimateUsageFromChars(promptChars: number, completionChars: number): GroqUsage {\n460\t  const promptTokens = Math.ceil(promptChars / FALLBACK_CHARS_PER_TOKEN);\n461\t  const completionTokens = Math.ceil(completionChars / FALLBACK_CHARS_PER_TOKEN);\n462\t  return {\n463\t    prompt_tokens: promptTokens,\n464\t    completion_tokens: completionTokens,\n465\t    total_tokens: promptTokens + completionTokens,\n466\t  };\n467\t}\n468\t\n469\texport function isGroqUsage(value: unknown): value is GroqUsage {\n470\t  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') {\n471\t    return false;\n472\t  }\n473\t\n474\t  const usage = value as GroqUsage;\n475\t  return typeof usage.prompt_tokens === 'number'\n476\t    && typeof usage.completion_tokens === 'number'\n477\t    && typeof usage.total_tokens === 'number';\n478\t}\n479\t\n480\t// =============================================================================\n481\t// CREDITS + FORMATTING\n482\t// =============================================================================\n483\t\n484\texport function usdToCredits(usd: number): number {\n485\t  if (!Number.isFinite(usd) || usd <= 0) {\n486\t    return 0.1;\n487\t  }\n488\t\n489\t  if (USD_PER_CREDIT <= 0) {\n490\t    return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(usd * 1000));\n491\t  }\n492\t\n493\t  return usd / USD_PER_CREDIT;\n494\t}\n495\t\n496\texport function creditsForCost(costUsd: number): number {\n497\t  if (!Number.isFinite(costUsd) || costUsd <= 0) {\n498\t    return 0.1;\n499\t  }\n500\t\n501\t  const rawCredits = usdToCredits(costUsd);\n502\t  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(rawCredits));\n503\t}\n504\t\n505\texport function estimateCreditsForCost(costUsd: number): number {\n506\t  if (!Number.isFinite(costUsd) || costUsd <= 0) {\n507\t    return 0;\n508\t  }\n509\t\n510\t  const rawCredits = usdToCredits(costUsd);\n511\t  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(rawCredits));\n512\t}\n513\t\n514\texport function roundUsd(value: number): number {\n515\t  return Math.round((value + Number.EPSILON) * 1_000_000) / 1_000_000;\n516\t}\n517\t\n518\texport function formatUsd(value: number): string {\n519\t  return roundUsd(value).toFixed(6);\n520\t}\n521\t"}]},"uuid":"daeda248-d9ab-4d08-8164-6f0f1b35a1d7","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:43:26.159Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/cost-calculator.ts","content":"// COST CALCULATION MODULE\n// Handles pricing calculations for STT providers and LLM post-processing\n\nimport { roundUpToTenth } from './utils';\n\n// =============================================================================\n// PRICING CONSTANTS\n// =============================================================================\n\n// ElevenLabs Scribe v2 Pricing (USD)\nconst ELEVENLABS_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.00983;\nconst ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE = 0.20;   // +20% on base when keyterm prompting is used (scribe_v2)\n\n// Deepgram Nova-3 Pricing (USD)\n// Base $0.0043 + features $0.0012\nconst DEEPGRAM_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.0055;\n\n// Groq Whisper Pricing (USD) — per-model: turbo is ~2.8x cheaper than large-v3.\n// https://groq.com/pricing — whisper-large-v3 $0.111/hr, turbo $0.04/hr.\nconst GROQ_WHISPER_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR = 0.111; // $0.111/hour (whisper-large-v3)\nconst GROQ_WHISPER_TURBO_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR = 0.04; // $0.04/hour (whisper-large-v3-turbo)\nconst GROQ_WHISPER_MIN_BILLABLE_SECONDS = 10;\n\n// xAI Grok STT Pricing (USD)\nconst XAI_STT_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR = 0.10;\n\n// Microsoft MAI-Transcribe 1.5 (Azure Speech) Pricing (USD)\nconst AZURE_MAI_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.006;\n\n// Google Cloud Speech-to-Text V2 Chirp 3 Pricing (USD)\nconst GOOGLE_CHIRP_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.016;\n\n// ── New HyperWhisper Cloud STT proxy providers (per-model) ──────────────────\n// Each provider exposes one or more models with distinct pricing. Token-billed\n// providers (OpenAI gpt-4o-*, Gemini) bill from the response `usage` object so\n// the charge is exact; duration-billed providers bill from returned audio\n// seconds. See shared-app-classification/cloud-stt-catalog.json for the\n// client-facing credits/min surface.\n\n// OpenAI — whisper-1 is duration-billed; gpt-4o-* are token-billed.\nconst OPENAI_WHISPER1_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.006;\nconst OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 2.50 / 1_000_000;\nconst OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_OUTPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 10.00 / 1_000_000;\nconst OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.25 / 1_000_000;\nconst OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_OUTPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 5.00 / 1_000_000;\n// Per-minute floors used only when the token `usage` object is missing/changed,\n// so a gpt-4o transcription never bills $0 (fail-closed).\nconst OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_FLOOR_PER_MINUTE = 0.006;\nconst OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_FLOOR_PER_MINUTE = 0.003;\n\n// Gemini — audio billed at 32 tokens/sec; cost read from usageMetadata. Input\n// is multi-modal: TEXT (the instruction+vocab prompt) and AUDIO bill at\n// DIFFERENT per-token rates on Flash models, so both must be tracked. Pro models\n// (2.5-pro, 3.1-pro) bill every input modality at one rate but switch to a\n// higher long-context tier above 200k prompt tokens. Rates: Standard paid tier,\n// per-1M ÷ 1e6. Ref: https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/pricing\ninterface GeminiRate {\n  textInputPerToken: number;\n  audioInputPerToken: number;\n  outputPerToken: number;\n  // Override rates for prompts above GEMINI_LONG_CONTEXT_THRESHOLD tokens.\n  // Present only for models with tiered (Pro) pricing; flat models omit it.\n  longContext?: { textInputPerToken: number; audioInputPerToken: number; outputPerToken: number };\n}\nconst GEMINI_LONG_CONTEXT_THRESHOLD = 200_000;\nconst M = 1_000_000;\nconst GEMINI_RATES: Record<string, GeminiRate> = {\n  'gemini-2.5-flash': { textInputPerToken: 0.30 / M, audioInputPerToken: 1.00 / M, outputPerToken: 2.50 / M },\n  'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': { textInputPerToken: 0.10 / M, audioInputPerToken: 0.30 / M, outputPerToken: 0.40 / M },\n  'gemini-2.5-pro': {\n    textInputPerToken: 1.25 / M, audioInputPerToken: 1.25 / M, outputPerToken: 10.00 / M,\n    longContext: { textInputPerToken: 2.50 / M, audioInputPerToken: 2.50 / M, outputPerToken: 15.00 / M },\n  },\n  'gemini-3-flash-preview': { textInputPerToken: 0.50 / M, audioInputPerToken: 1.00 / M, outputPerToken: 3.00 / M },\n  'gemini-3.1-pro-preview': {\n    textInputPerToken: 2.00 / M, audioInputPerToken: 2.00 / M, outputPerToken: 12.00 / M,\n    longContext: { textInputPerToken: 4.00 / M, audioInputPerToken: 4.00 / M, outputPerToken: 18.00 / M },\n  },\n};\nconst GEMINI_FALLBACK_RATE = GEMINI_RATES['gemini-2.5-flash'];\n\n// AssemblyAI — duration-billed; medical is a +$0.15/hr add-on, not a model.\nconst ASSEMBLYAI_UNIVERSAL2_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.15 / 60;       // $0.0025/min\nconst ASSEMBLYAI_UNIVERSAL3_PRO_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.21 / 60;   // $0.0035/min\nconst ASSEMBLYAI_MEDICAL_ADDON_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.15 / 60;    // +$0.0025/min\nconst ASSEMBLYAI_KEYTERMS_ADDON_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.05 / 60;   // +~$0.05/hr (universal-3-pro only)\n// AssemblyAI's separate sync product (<=120s clips, single blocking request)\n// always runs universal-3-5-pro, a model the async tiers above don't cover —\n// billed at its own published rate, \"the same rate as Universal-3.5 Pro\n// Realtime\". No medical/keyterms add-ons: the sync API doesn't support them.\n// Exported as the single source of truth for this rate: stt-models.ts's\n// preflight reservation figure (`ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE`)\n// imports it rather than hardcoding a second copy of the same literal, so the\n// amount actually reserved can never silently drift from what's actually billed.\nexport const ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.45 / 60;             // $0.0075/min\n\n// Mistral Voxtral — per-audio-minute billing.\nconst MISTRAL_VOXTRAL_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.003;\n\n// Soniox — token-billed. The ≈ $0.10/hr async equivalence covers input-AUDIO +\n// OUTPUT-text tokens for typical speech; custom-context INPUT-text tokens are\n// charged ON TOP at $3.50/1M (async). Soniox bills ~0.3 tokens per character.\n// Ref: https://soniox.com/pricing\nconst SONIOX_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE = 0.10 / 60;                       // ≈ $0.001667/min\nconst SONIOX_INPUT_TEXT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 3.50 / 1_000_000;            // async context tokens\nconst SONIOX_TOKENS_PER_CHAR = 0.3;\nexport function estimateSonioxContextTokens(contextText: string | undefined): number {\n  if (!contextText) return 0;\n  return Math.ceil(contextText.length * SONIOX_TOKENS_PER_CHAR);\n}\n\n// Anthropic Claude Haiku 4.5 Pricing (USD)\nconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.00 / 1_000_000;\nconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 5.00 / 1_000_000;\n// Prompt caching: writes bill at 1.25x input, reads at 0.10x input (5-minute TTL).\nconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_WRITE_COST_PER_TOKEN = ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN * 1.25;\nconst ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_READ_COST_PER_TOKEN = ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN * 0.10;\n\n// Cerebras GPT-OSS-120B Pricing (USD)\nconst CEREBRAS_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.35 / 1_000_000;\nconst CEREBRAS_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.75 / 1_000_000;\n\n// Groq GPT-OSS-120B Pricing (USD)\nconst GROQ_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.15 / 1_000_000;\nconst GROQ_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 0.60 / 1_000_000;\n\n// xAI Grok 4.1 Fast Pricing (USD)\nconst XAI_GROK_FAST_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN = 1.25 / 1_000_000;\nconst XAI_GROK_FAST_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN = 2.50 / 1_000_000;\n\n// ── HyperWhisper Cloud post-processing LLM providers (per-model) ────────────\n// Each provider exposes one or more chat models with distinct input/output\n// token pricing. These MUST match shared-app-classification/cloud-pp-catalog.json\n// (the display source of truth) — they are the ACTUAL billing constants. Rates\n// are USD per-1M ÷ 1e6. Re-confirmed against live provider pricing 2026-06-19.\ninterface LLMChatRate {\n  promptPerToken: number;\n  completionPerToken: number;\n}\n\n// OpenAI GPT-5 family (chat/completions). https://platform.openai.com/docs/pricing\nconst OPENAI_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n  'gpt-5-mini': { promptPerToken: 0.25 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 2.00 / 1_000_000 },\n  'gpt-5-nano': { promptPerToken: 0.05 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.40 / 1_000_000 },\n};\nconst OPENAI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'gpt-5-mini';\n\n// Google Gemini (OpenAI-compatible endpoint). https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/pricing\nconst GEMINI_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n  'gemini-2.5-flash': { promptPerToken: 0.30 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 2.50 / 1_000_000 },\n  'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': { promptPerToken: 0.10 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.40 / 1_000_000 },\n};\nconst GEMINI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'gemini-2.5-flash';\n\n// Mistral (chat/completions). https://mistral.ai/pricing + docs.mistral.ai\n// `mistral-small-latest` resolves to Mistral Small 4: the model-specific docs\n// list $0.15/$0.60, which conflicts with the lower pricing-tile figure — we\n// bill at the higher (model-docs) rate, the billing-safe choice. `mistral-nemo`\n// is not a valid chat id; the canonical API id is `open-mistral-nemo`.\n// Re-confirm both against live provider pages before secrets go live.\nconst MISTRAL_CHAT_RATES: Record<string, LLMChatRate> = {\n  'mistral-small-latest': { promptPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.60 / 1_000_000 },\n  'open-mistral-nemo': { promptPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000, completionPerToken: 0.15 / 1_000_000 },\n};\nconst MISTRAL_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL = 'mistral-small-latest';\n\n// Credit model: 1 credit = $0.001\nconst USD_PER_CREDIT = 0.001;\n\n// =============================================================================\n// TYPES\n// =============================================================================\n\nexport interface GroqUsage {\n  prompt_tokens: number;\n  completion_tokens: number;\n  total_tokens: number;\n}\n\n// =============================================================================\n// STT COSTS\n// =============================================================================\n\nexport function computeElevenLabsTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number, keyterms: boolean = false): number {\n  const durationMinutes = durationSeconds / 60;\n  const base = durationMinutes * ELEVENLABS_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n  // ElevenLabs keyterm prompting (scribe_v2) carries a documented +20% surcharge\n  // on the base transcription cost.\n  const raw = keyterms ? base * (1 + ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE) : base;\n  return roundUsd(raw);\n}\n\nexport function computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n  const durationMinutes = durationSeconds / 60;\n  const raw = durationMinutes * DEEPGRAM_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n  return roundUsd(raw);\n}\n\nexport function computeGroqTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number, model?: string): number {\n  const billableSeconds = Math.max(durationSeconds, GROQ_WHISPER_MIN_BILLABLE_SECONDS);\n  // whisper-large-v3 bills at the higher rate; everything else (including the\n  // default whisper-large-v3-turbo) bills at the turbo rate (~2.8x cheaper).\n  const costPerHour = model === 'whisper-large-v3'\n    ? GROQ_WHISPER_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR\n    : GROQ_WHISPER_TURBO_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR;\n  const raw = (billableSeconds / 3600) * costPerHour;\n  return roundUsd(raw);\n}\n\nexport function computeXaiTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n  const raw = (durationSeconds / 3600) * XAI_STT_COST_PER_AUDIO_HOUR;\n  return roundUsd(raw);\n}\n\nexport function computeAzureMaiTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n  const durationMinutes = durationSeconds / 60;\n  const raw = durationMinutes * AZURE_MAI_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n  return roundUsd(raw);\n}\n\nexport function computeGoogleChirpTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n  const durationMinutes = durationSeconds / 60;\n  const raw = durationMinutes * GOOGLE_CHIRP_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n  return roundUsd(raw);\n}\n\n// ── New cloud STT proxy providers ───────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport interface OpenAITranscriptionUsage {\n  durationSeconds: number;\n  inputTokens?: number;\n  outputTokens?: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * OpenAI: `whisper-1` bills on audio duration; the `gpt-4o-*` transcribe models\n * bill on tokens from the response `usage` object. The gpt-4o `prompt` text\n * input tokens are billed at the (higher) audio-input rate — they're a handful\n * of tokens, so erring high there is both simpler and conservative.\n */\nexport function computeOpenAITranscriptionCost(model: string, usage: OpenAITranscriptionUsage): number {\n  if (model === 'whisper-1') {\n    return roundUsd((usage.durationSeconds / 60) * OPENAI_WHISPER1_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE);\n  }\n\n  const inputTokens = Math.max(0, usage.inputTokens ?? 0);\n  const outputTokens = Math.max(0, usage.outputTokens ?? 0);\n  const isMini = model === 'gpt-4o-mini-transcribe';\n\n  const tokenCost = isMini\n    ? inputTokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN\n      + outputTokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_OUTPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN\n    : inputTokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN\n      + outputTokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_OUTPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n\n  if (tokenCost > 0) {\n    return roundUsd(tokenCost);\n  }\n\n  // Missing/changed `usage` schema would otherwise bill $0 — fall back to a\n  // duration-based estimate so token-billed models fail closed, mirroring the\n  // LLM `estimateUsageFromChars` safety net.\n  const floorPerMinute = isMini\n    ? OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_FLOOR_PER_MINUTE\n    : OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_FLOOR_PER_MINUTE;\n  return roundUsd((usage.durationSeconds / 60) * floorPerMinute);\n}\n\nexport interface GeminiTranscriptionUsage {\n  audioInputTokens: number;\n  /**\n   * Non-audio prompt-input tokens (the instruction + vocabulary text we send).\n   * Billed at the model's text-input rate — NOT the audio rate. The adapter\n   * derives this as promptTokenCount − audioInputTokens so audio + text always\n   * sums to the documented input total (no double-count, no unbilled remainder).\n   */\n  textInputTokens?: number;\n  outputTokens: number;\n  /** Duration estimate (from payload size) used only if usageMetadata is absent. */\n  fallbackDurationSeconds?: number;\n}\n\n// Gemini represents 1 minute of audio as 1,920 input tokens (32 tokens/sec).\nconst GEMINI_AUDIO_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE = 1_920;\n\n/**\n * Gemini bills audio-input + text-input + output tokens from `usageMetadata`.\n * Text and audio input have different per-token rates on Flash models, so both\n * are billed at their own rate. Pro models switch to a higher long-context tier\n * once the prompt (audio + text input) exceeds 200k tokens. Output tokens\n * include any thinking tokens (only 2.5-flash/flash-lite can zero thinking;\n * Pro/3.x always charge some). If usageMetadata is missing we fall back to a\n * duration-based estimate so a transcription never bills $0 (fail-closed).\n */\nexport function computeGeminiTranscriptionCost(model: string, usage: GeminiTranscriptionUsage): number {\n  const rateDef = GEMINI_RATES[model] ?? GEMINI_FALLBACK_RATE;\n  const audioTokens = Math.max(0, usage.audioInputTokens);\n  const textTokens = Math.max(0, usage.textInputTokens ?? 0);\n  const outputTokens = Math.max(0, usage.outputTokens);\n\n  // Long-context tier is keyed on the total prompt (input) token count.\n  const promptTokens = audioTokens + textTokens;\n  const rate = (rateDef.longContext && promptTokens > GEMINI_LONG_CONTEXT_THRESHOLD)\n    ? rateDef.longContext\n    : rateDef;\n\n  const tokenCost = audioTokens * rate.audioInputPerToken\n    + textTokens * rate.textInputPerToken\n    + outputTokens * rate.outputPerToken;\n\n  if (tokenCost > 0) {\n    return roundUsd(tokenCost);\n  }\n\n  const seconds = Math.max(0, usage.fallbackDurationSeconds ?? 0);\n  return roundUsd((seconds / 60) * GEMINI_AUDIO_TOKENS_PER_MINUTE * rateDef.audioInputPerToken);\n}\n\n// ~4 chars/token — matches the Gemini adapter's prompt-text estimate.\nconst RESERVATION_CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 4;\n\n/**\n * Conservative USD for the prompt-text INPUT tokens a TOKEN-BILLED provider will\n * charge for `initial_prompt` (Gemini, all models; OpenAI gpt-4o-transcribe /\n * gpt-4o-mini-transcribe). Used ONLY by the preflight reservation so a large\n * vocabulary prompt on a short clip can't be deducted beyond what was reserved.\n * Duration-billed providers (whisper-1, deepgram/groq/grok/elevenlabs/azure/\n * soniox/mistral) don't bill prompt tokens → 0; the ElevenLabs /\n * AssemblyAI keyterm add-ons are reserved separately via the per-minute surcharge.\n */\nexport function estimatePromptInputReservationUsd(\n  provider: string,\n  model: string | undefined,\n  initialPrompt: string | undefined,\n): number {\n  if (!initialPrompt) return 0;\n  const tokens = Math.ceil(initialPrompt.length / RESERVATION_CHARS_PER_TOKEN);\n  if (provider === 'gemini') {\n    const rate = GEMINI_RATES[model ?? ''] ?? GEMINI_FALLBACK_RATE;\n    return tokens * rate.textInputPerToken;\n  }\n  if (provider === 'openai') {\n    // whisper-1 is duration-billed — no prompt-token charge. Default + the\n    // explicit gpt-4o-transcribe use the (more expensive) transcribe input rate.\n    if (model === 'whisper-1') return 0;\n    if (model === 'gpt-4o-mini-transcribe') return tokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_MINI_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n    return tokens * OPENAI_GPT4O_TRANSCRIBE_INPUT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  }\n  if (provider === 'soniox') {\n    // Soniox charges the custom-context terms as async input-text tokens on top\n    // of the audio/output blend. Its tokenizer (~0.3 tok/char) differs from the\n    // 4-chars/token heuristic above, so use the Soniox-specific estimate.\n    return estimateSonioxContextTokens(initialPrompt) * SONIOX_INPUT_TEXT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  }\n  return 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * AssemblyAI duration-billed; `medical` layers the +$0.15/hr add-on on top of\n * the chosen base model (universal-2 or universal-3-pro). `keyterms` layers the\n * ~$0.05/hr keyterms-prompt add-on, but ONLY for universal-3-pro — universal-2\n * keyterms are free/beta and must not be charged.\n */\nexport function computeAssemblyAITranscriptionCost(\n  durationSeconds: number,\n  model: string,\n  medical: boolean = false,\n  keyterms: boolean = false,\n): number {\n  const basePerMinute = model === 'universal-3-pro'\n    ? ASSEMBLYAI_UNIVERSAL3_PRO_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE\n    : ASSEMBLYAI_UNIVERSAL2_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n  const keytermsPerMinute = (keyterms && model === 'universal-3-pro')\n    ? ASSEMBLYAI_KEYTERMS_ADDON_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE\n    : 0;\n  const perMinute = basePerMinute\n    + (medical ? ASSEMBLYAI_MEDICAL_ADDON_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE : 0)\n    + keytermsPerMinute;\n  return roundUsd((durationSeconds / 60) * perMinute);\n}\n\nexport function computeAssemblyAISyncTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n  return roundUsd((durationSeconds / 60) * ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE);\n}\n\nexport function computeMistralTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number): number {\n  return roundUsd((durationSeconds / 60) * MISTRAL_VOXTRAL_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE);\n}\n\nexport function computeSonioxTranscriptionCost(durationSeconds: number, contextTextTokens: number = 0): number {\n  const audioAndOutput = (durationSeconds / 60) * SONIOX_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n  const contextCost = Math.max(0, contextTextTokens) * SONIOX_INPUT_TEXT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  return roundUsd(audioAndOutput + contextCost);\n}\n\n// =============================================================================\n// LLM COSTS\n// =============================================================================\n\nexport function computeAnthropicCost(\n  inputTokens: number,\n  outputTokens: number,\n  cacheCreationTokens: number = 0,\n  cacheReadTokens: number = 0,\n): number {\n  // `inputTokens` is the uncached input delta; cache buckets are billed separately.\n  const promptCost = inputTokens * ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  const completionCost = outputTokens * ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  const cacheWriteCost = cacheCreationTokens * ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_WRITE_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  const cacheReadCost = cacheReadTokens * ANTHROPIC_HAIKU_CACHE_READ_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost + cacheWriteCost + cacheReadCost);\n}\n\nexport function computeCerebrasChatCost(usage: GroqUsage): number {\n  const promptCost = usage.prompt_tokens * CEREBRAS_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  const completionCost = usage.completion_tokens * CEREBRAS_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost);\n}\n\nexport function computeGroqChatCost(usage: GroqUsage): number {\n  const promptCost = usage.prompt_tokens * GROQ_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  const completionCost = usage.completion_tokens * GROQ_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost);\n}\n\nexport function computeXaiGrokFastChatCost(usage: GroqUsage): number {\n  const promptCost = usage.prompt_tokens * XAI_GROK_FAST_PROMPT_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  const completionCost = usage.completion_tokens * XAI_GROK_FAST_COMPLETION_COST_PER_TOKEN;\n  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost);\n}\n\n// Per-model chat cost for the OpenAI-compatible PP providers. Unknown models\n// fall back to the provider default rate so a catalog/header drift never bills\n// $0 (fail-closed, matching the duration-based STT floors above).\nfunction computeChatCost(rates: Record<string, LLMChatRate>, defaultModel: string, model: string, usage: GroqUsage): number {\n  const rate = rates[model] ?? rates[defaultModel];\n  const promptCost = usage.prompt_tokens * rate.promptPerToken;\n  const completionCost = usage.completion_tokens * rate.completionPerToken;\n  return roundUsd(promptCost + completionCost);\n}\n\nexport function computeOpenAIChatCost(model: string, usage: GroqUsage): number {\n  return computeChatCost(OPENAI_CHAT_RATES, OPENAI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL, model, usage);\n}\n\nexport function computeGeminiChatCost(model: string, usage: GroqUsage): number {\n  return computeChatCost(GEMINI_CHAT_RATES, GEMINI_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL, model, usage);\n}\n\nexport function computeMistralChatCost(model: string, usage: GroqUsage): number {\n  return computeChatCost(MISTRAL_CHAT_RATES, MISTRAL_DEFAULT_CHAT_MODEL, model, usage);\n}\n\n// Fallback token estimate (~4 chars/token) for when a provider response omits\n// or changes the usage schema. Ensures vendor schema drift fails closed\n// (billed via estimate) instead of open (costUsd = 0).\nconst FALLBACK_CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 4;\n\nexport function estimateUsageFromChars(promptChars: number, completionChars: number): GroqUsage {\n  const promptTokens = Math.ceil(promptChars / FALLBACK_CHARS_PER_TOKEN);\n  const completionTokens = Math.ceil(completionChars / FALLBACK_CHARS_PER_TOKEN);\n  return {\n    prompt_tokens: promptTokens,\n    completion_tokens: completionTokens,\n    total_tokens: promptTokens + completionTokens,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function isGroqUsage(value: unknown): value is GroqUsage {\n  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') {\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  const usage = value as GroqUsage;\n  return typeof usage.prompt_tokens === 'number'\n    && typeof usage.completion_tokens === 'number'\n    && typeof usage.total_tokens === 'number';\n}\n\n// =============================================================================\n// CREDITS + FORMATTING\n// =============================================================================\n\nexport function usdToCredits(usd: number): number {\n  if (!Number.isFinite(usd) || usd <= 0) {\n    return 0.1;\n  }\n\n  if (USD_PER_CREDIT <= 0) {\n    return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(usd * 1000));\n  }\n\n  return usd / USD_PER_CREDIT;\n}\n\nexport function creditsForCost(costUsd: number): number {\n  if (!Number.isFinite(costUsd) || costUsd <= 0) {\n    return 0.1;\n  }\n\n  const rawCredits = usdToCredits(costUsd);\n  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(rawCredits));\n}\n\nexport function estimateCreditsForCost(costUsd: number): number {\n  if (!Number.isFinite(costUsd) || costUsd <= 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  const rawCredits = usdToCredits(costUsd);\n  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(rawCredits));\n}\n\nexport function roundUsd(value: number): number {\n  return Math.round((value + Number.EPSILON) * 1_000_000) / 1_000_000;\n}\n\nexport function formatUsd(value: number): string {\n  return roundUsd(value).toFixed(6);\n}\n","numLines":521,"startLine":1,"totalLines":521}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a7ab1459-c118-444b-90c7-66492d930866","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Currently only 'medical', which\n46\t   * AssemblyAI layers on a base model via `domain: \"medical-v1\"` (a metered\n47\t   * add-on, not a separate model). Providers that don't support it ignore it.\n48\t   */\n49\t  domain?: string;\n50\t}\n51\t\n52\t/**\n53\t * Why a provider attempt was deemed unavailable. Lets the route and dashboards\n54\t * distinguish \"the upstream was slow and we gave up\" (`timeout` — the request\n55\t * might have succeeded with more budget) from \"the upstream actually failed\"\n56\t * (`upstream_5xx` / `rate_limit`) and from \"we got a 2xx we couldn't use\"\n57\t * (`bad_response` — e.g. ElevenLabs' geo-block HTML or an empty gzip body),\n58\t * WITHOUT having to correlate a separate provider-level log line by requestId.\n59\t */\n60\texport type ProviderUnavailableKind =\n61\t  | 'timeout'        // our per-request budget elapsed; upstream may have succeeded given more time\n62\t  | 'network_error'  // connection failed/reset before any response\n63\t  | 'rate_limit'     // upstream 429\n64\t  | 'upstream_5xx'   // upstream 5xx server error\n65\t  | 'bad_response'   // 2xx with an unusable body (geo-block HTML, empty, non-JSON)\n66\t  | 'unknown';\n67\t\n68\t/**\n69\t * Thrown when a provider is temporarily unavailable (429, 403 edge block, etc.)\n70\t * Signals the fallback chain to try the next provider. `kind` carries the root\n71\t * cause and `status`/`elapsedMs` the upstream HTTP status and attempt latency\n72\t * when known, so the route can log the reason inline instead of dropping it.\n73\t */\n74\texport class ProviderUnavailableError extends Error {\n75\t  readonly kind: ProviderUnavailableKind;\n76\t  readonly status?: number;\n77\t  readonly elapsedMs?: number;\n78\t\n79\t  constructor(\n80\t    provider: string,\n81\t    reason: string,\n82\t    opts: { kind?: ProviderUnavailableKind; status?: number; elapsedMs?: number } = {},\n83\t  ) {\n84\t    super(`${provider} unavailable: ${reason}`);\n85\t    this.name = 'ProviderUnavailableError';\n86\t    this.kind = opts.kind ?? Undefined → use the requested model.\n   */\n  model?: string;\n}\n\nexport interface ProviderRequestContext {\n  requestId?: string;\n  attempt?: number;\n  /**\n   * Upstream model id the caller selected (e.g. 'gpt-4o-transcribe',\n   * 'universal-3-pro', 'nova-3-medical'). 'unknown';\n    this.status = opts.status;\n    this.elapsedMs = opts.elapsedMs;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Thrown when an upstream provider rejects the request input with a non-auth\n * 4xx (e.g. ElevenLabs 400 on a language code it doesn't accept, or a format\n * it can't decode). A sibling provider may well accept the same input, so the\n * transcribe route treats this like ProviderUnavailableError and continues\n * the fallback chain rather than failing the whole request. Distinct from\n * AudioTooLargeError / UnsupportedAudioFormatError, which are deterministic\n * across providers and map to a fixed client error. `status` is the upstream\n * HTTP status that triggered it.\n */\nexport class ProviderInputError extends Error {\n  readonly status: number;\n\n  constructor(provider: string, status: number, reason: string) {\n    super(`${provider} rejected input (${status}): ${reason}`);\n    this.name = 'ProviderInputError';\n    this.status = status;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Thrown when the audio payload exceeds an upstream provider's inline-content\n * cap and a long-file path (e.g. GCS upload) isn't available in v1. The\n * transcribe route turns this into a 413 to the client instead of retrying\n * through the fallback chain.\n */\nexport class AudioTooLargeError extends Error {\n  readonly actualBytes: number;\n  readonly maxBytes: number;\n\n  constructor(provider: string, actualBytes: number, maxBytes: number) {\n    super(`${provider} audio too large: ${actualBytes} bytes (max ${maxBytes})`);\n    this.name = 'AudioTooLargeError';\n    this.actualBytes = actualBytes;\n    this.maxBytes = maxBytes;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Thrown when the upstream provider does not accept the supplied audio\n * format and we can't transparently convert it. The transcribe route maps\n * this to HTTP 415 so the client can re-encode and retry, rather than\n * falling through the fallback chain.\n */\nexport class UnsupportedAudioFormatError extends Error {\n  readonly contentType: string;\n  readonly acceptedFormats: readonly string[];\n\n  constructor(provider: string, contentType: string, acceptedFormats: readonly string[]) {\n    super(`${provider} does not accept ${contentType}; accepts ${acceptedFormats.join(', ')}`);\n    this.name = 'UnsupportedAudioFormatError';\n    this.contentType = contentType;\n    this.acceptedFormats = acceptedFormats;\n  }\n}\n","numLines":147,"startLine":1,"totalLines":147}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"280a28f4-dc7b-407e-8f16-253d129bcb91","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"ad0b9123-f5cc-4084-bf38-40eaa404feae","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"65ea576b-6c0e-4785-96fe-755d0d4890b3","agentId":"a1f765d637aca55e2","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011rzZkR4iN2fGJrJHAjAB6P","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// SERVER-SIDE STT MODEL REGISTRY\n2\t// Single source of truth for which (provider, model) pairs HyperWhisper Cloud\n3\t// will route to, their preflight credit rate, preview status, and vocabulary\n4\t// support. The transcribe route validates every request against this registry\n5\t// BEFORE spending any upstream money, so an unknown or spoofed provider/model\n6\t// is rejected with a 400 rather than silently falling back to a paid default.\n7\t//\n8\t// The client-facing catalog (credits/min captions, language lists, accuracy\n9\t// tiers) lives in `shared-app-classification/cloud-stt-catalog.json`. This file\n10\t// is the backend's narrower, security-critical view: what is actually routable\n11\t// and how to meter it.\n12\t\n13\timport { ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE } from './cost-calculator';\n14\t\n15\texport type SttProviderId =\n16\t  | 'deepgram'\n17\t  | 'groq'\n18\t  | 'elevenlabs'\n19\t  | 'grok'\n20\t  | 'azure-mai'\n21\t  | 'google-chirp'\n22\t  | 'openai'\n23\t  | 'gemini'\n24\t  | 'assemblyai'\n25\t  | 'mistral'\n26\t  | 'soniox';\n27\t\n28\texport interface SttModelDef {\n29\t  /** Upstream model id. Empty string for single-model providers (grok). */\n30\t  id: string;\n31\t  /** Marks a preview/experimental model so clients can badge it. */\n32\t  isPreview?: boolean;\n33\t  /** Whether the upstream honours custom-vocabulary / keyterm biasing. */\n34\t  supportsVocabulary: boolean;\n35\t  /**\n36\t   * Conservative USD-per-audio-minute figure used ONLY for the preflight\n37\t   * credit reservation. Actual billing is computed from the upstream response\n38\t   * (token usage or returned audio seconds) in the provider adapter, so this\n39\t   * just has to be a safe upper-ish bound to gate low-balance abuse.\n40\t   */\n41\t  estimatedUsdPerMinute: number;\n42\t}\n43\t\n44\texport interface SttProviderDef {\n45\t  id: SttProviderId;\n46\t  /** Model used when the caller omits an explicit model. */\n47\t  defaultModel: string;\n48\t  /**\n49\t   * Self-only providers never fall back to a sibling: the caller picked this\n50\t   * model for a reason, so on failure we surface an error rather than silently\n51\t   * substituting a different model/price. An unrecognised model is\n233\t * rejected (fail-closed) — the route turns this into a 400.\n234\t */\n235\texport function resolveModel(provider: SttProviderId, requested?: string): ModelResolution {\n236\t  const def = PROVIDERS[provider];\n237\t  const validModels = def.models.map((m) => m.id);\n238\t  const trimmed = (requested ?? '').trim();\n239\t\n240\t  // No model supplied → provider default.\n241\t  if (trimmed.length === 0) {\n242\t    const fallback = def.models.find((m) => m.id === def.defaultModel) ?? def.models[0];\n243\t    return { ok: true, model: fallback };\n244\t  }\n245\t\n246\t  const match = def.models.find((m) => m.id === trimmed);\n247\t  if (match) {\n248\t    return { ok: true, model: match };\n249\t  }\n250\t\n251\t  return {\n252\t    ok: false,\n253\t    reason: `Model \"${trimmed}\" is not available for provider \"${provider}\"`,\n254\t    validModels,\n255\t  };\n256\t}\n257\t\n258\t/**\n259\t * Preflight USD/min for a (provider, model), including the medical add-on where\n260\t * the provider meters it. Used only for the credit reservation; actual cost is\n261\t * computed from the upstream response in the adapter.\n262\t */\n263\texport function estimatedUsdPerMinute(\n264\t  provider: SttProviderId,\n265\t  model?: string,\n266\t  medical: boolean = false,\n267\t  keyterms: boolean = false,\n268\t): number {\n269\t  const resolution = resolveModel(provider, model);\n270\t  const base = resolution.ok\n271\t    ? resolution.model.estimatedUsdPerMinute\n272\t    : PROVIDERS[provider].models[0].estimatedUsdPerMinute;\n273\t\n274\t  if (provider === 'elevenlabs') {\n275\t    // Keyterm prompting adds +20% on base, scribe_v2 only (scribe_v1 has no biasing).\n276\t    const resolvedModel = resolution.ok ? resolution.model.id : PROVIDERS.elevenlabs.models[0].id;\n277\t    return (keyterms && resolvedModel === 'scribe_v2') ? base * (1 + ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE) : base;\n278\t  }\n279\t\n280\t  if (provider !== 'assemblyai') {\n281\t    return base;\n282\t  }\n283\t\n284\t  const resolvedModel = resolution.ok ? resolution.model.id : PROVIDERS.assemblyai.models[0].id;\n285\t  const medicalAddon = medical ? The transcribe route validates every request against this registry\n// BEFORE spending any upstream money, so an unknown or spoofed provider/model\n// is rejected with a 400 rather than silently falling back to a paid default.\n//\n// The client-facing catalog (credits/min captions, language lists, accuracy\n// tiers) lives in `shared-app-classification/cloud-stt-catalog.json`. This file\n// is the backend's narrower, security-critical view: what is actually routable\n// and how to meter it.\n\nimport { ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE } from './cost-calculator';\n\nexport type SttProviderId =\n  | 'deepgram'\n  | 'groq'\n  | 'elevenlabs'\n  | 'grok'\n  | 'azure-mai'\n  | 'google-chirp'\n  | 'openai'\n  | 'gemini'\n  | 'assemblyai'\n  | 'mistral'\n  | 'soniox';\n\nexport interface SttModelDef {\n  /** Upstream model id. Empty string for single-model providers (grok). */\n  id: string;\n  /** Marks a preview/experimental model so clients can badge it. */\n  isPreview?: boolean;\n  /** Whether the upstream honours custom-vocabulary / keyterm biasing. */\n  supportsVocabulary: boolean;\n  /**\n   * Conservative USD-per-audio-minute figure used ONLY for the preflight\n   * credit reservation. Actual billing is computed from the upstream response\n   * (token usage or returned audio seconds) in the provider adapter, so this\n   * just has to be a safe upper-ish bound to gate low-balance abuse.\n   */\n  estimatedUsdPerMinute: number;\n}\n\nexport interface SttProviderDef {\n  id: SttProviderId;\n  /** Model used when the caller omits an explicit model. */\n  defaultModel: string;\n  /**\n   * Self-only providers never fall back to a sibling: the caller picked this\n   * model for a reason, so on failure we surface an error rather than silently\n   * substituting a different model/price. All new proxy providers are self-only;\n   * the original cheap trio (deepgram/groq/elevenlabs) keep cross-fallback.\n   */\n  selfOnly: boolean;\n  /** Whether this provider's transcription flow is async (upload + poll). */\n  async: boolean;\n  models: SttModelDef[];\n}\n\n// Medical add-on multiplier surface — only AssemblyAI meters it today.\nexport const MEDICAL_DOMAIN = 'medical';\nconst ASSEMBLYAI_MEDICAL_ADDON_USD_PER_MINUTE = 0.15 / 60;\n// Keyterms add-on ($0.05/hr) applies only to universal-3-pro; universal-2 is free/beta.\nconst ASSEMBLYAI_KEYTERMS_ADDON_USD_PER_MINUTE = 0.05 / 60;\n// AssemblyAI's separate sync product (<=120s clips, single blocking request)\n// always runs universal-3-5-pro at its own published rate (\"the same rate as\n// Universal-3.5 Pro Realtime\") — HIGHER than either async tier below and not\n// modeled by a `models[]` entry (sync isn't a selectable model; it's a\n// routing decision the sync-eligibility gate makes for a request that could\n// have asked for either async model). Exported so the preflight reservation\n// (estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks in routes/transcribe.ts) can reserve\n// against it for a request that could route through sync, instead of only\n// ever reserving the lower async catalog rate for the requested model — a\n// short clip (sync's target case) must not be able to deduct more than was\n// reserved. Re-exports cost-calculator.ts's `ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE`\n// (the single source of truth for this rate, used for ACTUAL billing) instead\n// of a second hardcoded copy of the same literal, so the reserved amount can\n// never silently drift from what's actually billed.\nexport const ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE = ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n// ElevenLabs keyterm prompting carries a +20% surcharge on base (scribe_v2 only).\nconst ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE = 0.20;\n\nconst PROVIDERS: Record<SttProviderId, SttProviderDef> = {\n  // ── Original cheap trio: cross-provider fallback retained ──\n  deepgram: {\n    id: 'deepgram',\n    defaultModel: 'nova-3-general',\n    selfOnly: false,\n    async: false,\n    models: [\n      { id: 'nova-3-general', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n      { id: 'nova-3-medical', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n      { id: 'nova-2-general', An unrecognised model is\n * rejected (fail-closed) — the route turns this into a 400.\n */\nexport function resolveModel(provider: SttProviderId, requested?: string): ModelResolution {\n  const def = PROVIDERS[provider];\n  const validModels = def.models.map((m) => m.id);\n  const trimmed = (requested ?? '').trim();\n\n  // No model supplied → provider default.\n  if (trimmed.length === 0) {\n    const fallback = def.models.find((m) => m.id === def.defaultModel) ?? def.models[0];\n    return { ok: true, model: fallback };\n  }\n\n  const match = def.models.find((m) => m.id === trimmed);\n  if (match) {\n    return { ok: true, model: match };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    ok: false,\n    reason: `Model \"${trimmed}\" is not available for provider \"${provider}\"`,\n    validModels,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Preflight USD/min for a (provider, model), including the medical add-on where\n * the provider meters it. Used only for the credit reservation; actual cost is\n * computed from the upstream response in the adapter.\n */\nexport function estimatedUsdPerMinute(\n  provider: SttProviderId,\n  model?: string,\n  medical: boolean = false,\n  keyterms: boolean = false,\n): number {\n  const resolution = resolveModel(provider, model);\n  const base = resolution.ok\n    ? resolution.model.estimatedUsdPerMinute\n    : PROVIDERS[provider].models[0].estimatedUsdPerMinute;\n\n  if (provider === 'elevenlabs') {\n    // Keyterm prompting adds +20% on base, scribe_v2 only (scribe_v1 has no biasing).\n    const resolvedModel = resolution.ok ? resolution.model.id : PROVIDERS.elevenlabs.models[0].id;\n    return (keyterms && resolvedModel === 'scribe_v2') ? base * (1 + ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE) : base;\n  }\n\n  if (provider !== 'assemblyai') {\n    return base;\n  }\n\n  const resolvedModel = resolution.ok ? resolution.model.id : PROVIDERS.assemblyai.models[0].id;\n  const medicalAddon = medical ? ASSEMBLYAI_MEDICAL_ADDON_USD_PER_MINUTE : 0;\n  // Keyterms add-on only applies to universal-3-pro (free/beta on universal-2).\n  const keytermsAddon = (keyterms && resolvedModel === 'universal-3-pro')\n    ? ASSEMBLYAI_KEYTERMS_ADDON_USD_PER_MINUTE\n    : 0;\n  return base + medicalAddon + keytermsAddon;\n}\n","numLines":292,"startLine":1,"totalLines":292}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ad0b9123-f5cc-4084-bf38-40eaa404feae","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
