{"parentUuid":"47ee14f0-fb3e-4386-8a02-a4e88c353758","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"65ea576b-6c0e-4785-96fe-755d0d4890b3","agentId":"a1f765d637aca55e2","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PqmntXFVNbjWnuVJd7B4qV","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// TRANSCRIPTION ROUTE\n2\t// POST /transcribe - Main transcription endpoint\n3\t// Supports multiple STT providers with automatic fallback\n4\t\n5\timport type { Context } from 'hono';\n6\timport { transcribeWithDeepgram } from '../providers/deepgram';\n7\timport { transcribeWithGroq } from '../providers/groq';\n8\timport { transcribeWithElevenLabs } from '../providers/elevenlabs';\n9\timport { transcribeWithXaiGrok } from '../providers/xai-stt';\n10\timport { transcribeWithAzureMai } from '../providers/azure-mai';\n11\timport { transcribeWithGoogleChirp } from '../providers/google-chirp';\n12\timport { transcribeWithOpenAI } from '../providers/openai';\n13\timport { transcribeWithGemini } from '../providers/gemini';\n14\timport {\n15\t  transcribeWithAssemblyAI,\n16\t  hasExplicitLanguage as hasExplicitAssemblyAILanguage,\n17\t  SYNC_ELIGIBLE_ESTIMATED_SECONDS as ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ELIGIBLE_ESTIMATED_SECONDS,\n18\t} from '../providers/assemblyai';\n19\timport { transcribeWithMistral } from '../providers/mistral';\n20\timport { transcribeWithSoniox } from '../providers/soniox';\n21\timport type { ProviderRequestContext, TranscriptionResult } from '../providers/types';\n22\timport { AudioTooLargeError, ProviderInputError, ProviderUnavailableError, UnsupportedAudioFormatError } from '../providers/types';\n23\timport { creditsForCost, estimatePromptInputReservationUsd, formatUsd } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\n24\timport {\n25\t  estimatedUsdPerMinute,\n26\t  getProviderDef,\n27\t  isValidProviderId,\n28\t  resolveModel,\n29\t  MEDICAL_DOMAIN,\n30\t  ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE,\n31\t  type SttProviderId,\n32\t} from '../lib/stt-models';\n33\timport { generateRequestId, getClientIP, getFlyRequestId } from '../lib/request-id';\n34\timport { rawQuery } from '../lib/query';\n35\timport {\n36\t  FLY_REPLAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES,\n37\t  GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES,\n38\t  GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES,\n39\t  MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES,\n40\t  OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES,\n41\t} from '../lib/constants';\n42\timport { isIPBlocked } from '../lib/redis';\n43\timport {\n44\t  errorResponse,\n45\t  fileTooLargeResponse,\n46\t  invalidContentTypeResponse,\n47\t  missingContentLengthResponse,\n48\t} from '../lib/responses';\n49\timport { validateAuth } from '../middleware/auth';\n50\timport { deductCredits, estimateAudioSecondsFromSize, validateCredits } from '../middleware/credits';\n51\timport { flyProxyOverheadMs, logEvent, machineUptimeMs } from '../lib/logging';\n52\t\n53\t// Supported providers (mirror the server-side registry in lib/stt-models.ts).\n54\texport type Provider = SttProviderId;\n55\t\n56\t// Fly regions where ElevenLabs serves a text/html FAQ page instead of JSON\n57\t// (geo-block on Japan + India confirmed via per-region smoke 2026-06-07).\n58\t// Requests landing here are replayed to `iad` before any work happens.\n59\tconst ELEVENLABS_BLOCKED_FLY_REGIONS = new Set(['nrt', 'bom', 'maa']);\n60\tconst ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION = 'iad';\n61\t\n62\t// Human-readable base label per provider. The model is appended at runtime via\n63\t// formatProviderName() so the response header / metering reflects exactly which\n64\t// model ran (e.g. \"deepgram/nova-3-medical\", \"openai/gpt-4o-transcribe\").\n65\tconst PROVIDER_NAMES: Record<Provider, string> = {\n66\t  deepgram: 'deepgram',\n67\t  elevenlabs: 'elevenlabs',\n68\t  groq: 'groq',\n69\t  grok: 'xai-grok',\n70\t  'azure-mai': 'azure-mai',\n71\t  'google-chirp': 'google-chirp',\n72\t  openai: 'openai',\n73\t  gemini: 'gemini',\n74\t  assemblyai: 'assemblyai',\n75\t  mistral: 'mistral',\n76\t  soniox: 'soniox',\n77\t};\n78\t\n79\tfunction formatProviderName(provider: Provider, model: string): string {\n80\t  const base = PROVIDER_NAMES[provider];\n81\t  return model ? `${base}/${model}` : base;\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\t// Fallback chains: the original cheap trio (plus grok) cascade through\n85\t// alternatives — ElevenLabs (most expensive) is the last resort. Every other\n86\t// provider is SELF-ONLY: the caller picked that specific model, so on failure\n87\t// we surface an error rather than silently substituting a different model and\n88\t// price. (A cross-provider fallback would also change the metered cost.)\n89\tconst FALLBACK_CHAINS: Record<Provider, Provider[]> = {\n90\t  elevenlabs: ['elevenlabs', 'deepgram', 'groq'],\n91\t  groq: ['groq', 'deepgram', 'elevenlabs'],\n92\t  deepgram: ['deepgram', 'groq', 'elevenlabs'],\n93\t  grok: ['grok', 'deepgram', 'groq', 'elevenlabs'],\n94\t  'azure-mai': ['azure-mai'],\n95\t  'google-chirp': ['google-chirp'],\n96\t  openai: ['openai'],\n97\t  gemini: ['gemini'],\n98\t  assemblyai: ['assemblyai'],\n99\t  mistral: ['mistral'],\n100\t  soniox: ['soniox'],\n101\t};\n102\t\n103\tconst PROVIDER_FN: Record<Provider, (\n104\t  audio: ArrayBuffer,\n105\t  contentType: string,\n106\t  language?: string,\n107\t  initialPrompt?: string,\n108\t  context?: ProviderRequestContext,\n109\t) => Promise<TranscriptionResult>> = {\n110\t  deepgram: transcribeWithDeepgram,\n111\t  groq: transcribeWithGroq,\n112\t  elevenlabs: transcribeWithElevenLabs,\n113\t  grok: transcribeWithXaiGrok,\n114\t  'azure-mai': transcribeWithAzureMai,\n115\t  'google-chirp': transcribeWithGoogleChirp,\n116\t  openai: transcribeWithOpenAI,\n117\t  gemini: transcribeWithGemini,\n118\t  assemblyai: transcribeWithAssemblyAI,\n119\t  mistral: transcribeWithMistral,\n120\t  soniox: transcribeWithSoniox,\n121\t};\n122\t\n123\t/**\n124\t * Preflight credit reservation. For the primary provider we estimate against\n125\t * the chosen model (and medical add-on); for fallback siblings we estimate\n126\t * against their default model. The reservation uses the most expensive member\n127\t * of the chain so we never under-reserve. `model`/`medical` are optional to\n128\t * keep the historical 2-arg call signature working.\n129\t */\n130\texport function estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks(\n131\t  sizeBytes: number,\n132\t  provider: Provider,\n133\t  model?: string,\n134\t  medical: boolean = false,\n135\t  initialPrompt?: string,\n136\t  language?: string,\n137\t): number {\n138\t  const chain = FALLBACK_CHAINS[provider];\n139\t  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n140\t  const hasInitialPrompt = Boolean(initialPrompt);\n141\t  const rates = chain.map((p) => estimatedUsdPerMinute(\n142\t    p,\n143\t    p === provider ? model : undefined,\n144\t    p === provider ? medical : false,\n145\t    // The keyterm surcharge is billed by ANY chain member that supports it\n146\t    // (ElevenLabs scribe_v2 / AssemblyAI universal-3-pro) whenever an\n147\t    // initial_prompt is present — not just the primary provider. A\n148\t    // Deepgram→ElevenLabs fallback still forwards initial_prompt and bills the\n149\t    // +20% surcharge, so reserve for it on every eligible sibling. Other\n150\t    // providers ignore the flag (estimatedUsdPerMinute scopes the add-on), so\n151\t    // this never over-reserves for, say, a Deepgram-only success path.\n152\t    hasInitialPrompt && (p === 'elevenlabs' || p === 'assemblyai'),\n153\t  ));\n154\t  // AssemblyAI's sync fast path (<120s, non-medical, EXPLICIT-language clips)\n155\t  // always runs universal-3-5-pro at its OWN higher published rate — not the\n156\t  // async catalog rate for the requested model (universal-2 / universal-3-pro),\n157\t  // which `estimatedUsdPerMinute` above reserves against. A short clip is\n158\t  // exactly sync's target case, so without this a short, non-medical\n159\t  // AssemblyAI request could be deducted for more than was reserved. This\n160\t  // condition must exactly mirror `transcribeWithAssemblyAI`'s real\n161\t  // eligibility gate (medical + duration + explicit language) — reusing\n162\t  // `hasExplicitLanguage` rather than reimplementing it here, since an\n163\t  // auto-language request never actually routes through sync (sync has no\n164\t  // auto-detect) and over-reserving for it could wrongly reject a low-balance\n165\t  // user at preflight for a request that will only ever go through the\n166\t  // cheaper async path.\n167\t  if (\n168\t    provider === 'assemblyai' && !medical\n169\t    && estimatedSeconds < ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ELIGIBLE_ESTIMATED_SECONDS\n170\t    && hasExplicitAssemblyAILanguage(language)\n171\t  ) {\n172\t    rates.push(ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE);\n173\t  }\n174\t  const usdPerMinute = Math.max(...rates);\n175\t  // Token-billed providers (Gemini, OpenAI gpt-4o*) charge the prompt text as\n176\t  // input tokens on top of the audio. Reserve that flat cost for the primary\n177\t  // provider (these are self-only chains) so a large vocabulary prompt on a\n178\t  // short clip can't be deducted beyond what was reserved.\n179\t  const promptReservationUsd = estimatePromptInputReservationUsd(provider, model, initialPrompt);\n180\t  const estimatedCostUsd = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * usdPerMinute + promptReservationUsd;\n181\t  return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(estimatedCostUsd));\n182\t}\n183\t\n184\ttype ProviderSelection =\n185\t  | { ok: true; provider: Provider }\n186\t  | { ok: false; provided: string };\n187\t\n188\tfunction extractProvider(c: Context): ProviderSelection {\n189\t  const header = c.req.header('X-STT-Provider')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n190\t  // No header → historical default (many clients send only a provider, some\n191\t  // none). An explicitly-supplied but unknown provider is REJECTED (fail-closed)\n192\t  // rather than silently billed against a default upstream.\n193\t  if (!header) {\n194\t    return { ok: true, provider: 'deepgram' };\n195\t  }\n196\t  if (isValidProviderId(header)) {\n197\t    return { ok: true, provider: header };\n198\t  }\n199\t  return { ok: false, provided: header };\n200\t}\n201\t\n202\tfunction extractModel(c: Context): string | undefined {\n203\t  return c.req.header('X-STT-Model')?.trim() || rawQuery(c.req.url, 'model')?.trim() || undefined;\n204\t}\n205\t\n206\tfunction extractDomain(c: Context): string | undefined {\n207\t  const domain = c.req.header('X-STT-Domain')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n208\t  return domain || undefined;\n209\t}\n210\t\n211\tfunction validateStreamingHeaders(c: Context, provider: Provider):\n212\t  | { ok: true; contentType: string; contentLength: number }\n213\t  | { ok: false; response: Response } {\n214\t  const contentType = c.req.header('Content-Type') || '';\n215\t  if (!contentType.startsWith('audio/')) {\n216\t    return { ok: false, response: invalidContentTypeResponse('audio/*', contentType) };\n217\t  }\n218\t\n219\t  const contentLengthHeader = c.req.header('Content-Length');\n220\t  if (!contentLengthHeader) {\n221\t    return { ok: false, response: missingContentLengthResponse() };\n222\t  }\n223\t\n224\t  const contentLength = Number.parseInt(contentLengthHeader, 10);\n225\t  if (!Number.isFinite(contentLength) || contentLength <= 0) {\n226\t    return { ok: false, response: errorResponse(400, 'Invalid Content-Length', 'Content-Length must be a positive integer') };\n227\t  }\n228\t\n229\t  if (contentLength > MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES) {\n230\t    return { ok: false, response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES) };\n231\t  }\n232\t\n233\t  // Google Chirp inline cap (~9.5 MB) applies before we buffer the body —\n234\t  // without a scratch GCS bucket the provider has no path for larger audio,\n235\t  // and we don't want to allocate a 50 MB buffer just to 413 the caller.\n236\t  if (\n237\t    provider === 'google-chirp'\n238\t    && contentLength > GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES\n239\t    && !(process.env.GOOGLE_SPEECH_GCS_BUCKET || '').trim()\n240\t  ) {\n241\t    return {\n242\t      ok: false,\n243\t      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n244\t    };\n245\t  }\n246\t\n247\t  // Gemini sends audio inline (base64) and rejects anything over ~14 MB raw.\n248\t  // Gate on Content-Length before buffering so an oversized upload is rejected\n249\t  // early instead of after buffering up to MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES on the machine.\n250\t  if (provider === 'gemini' && contentLength > GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES) {\n251\t    return {\n252\t      ok: false,\n253\t      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n254\t    };\n255\t  }\n256\t\n257\t  // OpenAI hard-rejects audio over 25 MB with a 400. Gate on Content-Length\n258\t  // before buffering so we return 413 without allocating the buffer first.\n259\t  if (provider === 'openai' && contentLength > OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES) {\n260\t    return {\n261\t      ok: false,\n262\t      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n263\t    };\n264\t  }\n265\t\n266\t  return { ok: true, contentType, contentLength };\n267\t}\n268\t\n269\texport async function transcribeRoute(c: Context) {\n270\t  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n271\t  const startTime = performance.now();\n272\t  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n273\t  const flyRequestId = getFlyRequestId(c);\n274\t\n275\t  // IP block check\n276\t  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n277\t    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n278\t      reason: 'ip_blocked',\n279\t      flyRequestId,\n280\t    });\n281\t    return errorResponse(403, 'Access denied', 'Your IP has been temporarily blocked due to abuse');\n282\t  }\n283\t  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.ip_check_done', { flyRequestId });\n284\t\n285\t  const providerSelection = extractProvider(c);\n286\t  if (!providerSelection.ok) {\n287\t    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n288\t      reason: 'invalid_provider',\n289\t      flyRequestId,\n290\t      provided: providerSelection.provided,\n291\t    });\n292\t    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid STT provider',\n293\t      `Unknown X-STT-Provider \"${providerSelection.provided}\".`,\n294\t      { requestId, provided: providerSelection.provided },\n295\t    );\n296\t  }\n297\t  const provider = providerSelection.provider;\n298\t\n299\t  // Resolve + validate the requested model against the server-side registry.\n300\t  // An unknown model for the provider is rejected (fail-closed) rather than\n301\t  // silently routed to the provider default at a possibly different price.\n302\t  const requestedModel = extractModel(c);\n303\t  const modelResolution = resolveModel(provider, requestedModel);\n304\t  if (!modelResolution.ok) {\n305\t    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n306\t      reason: 'invalid_model',\n307\t      flyRequestId,\n308\t      provider,\n309\t      requestedModel,\n310\t    });\n311\t    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid STT model', modelResolution.reason, {\n312\t      requestId,\n313\t      provider,\n314\t      requested_model: requestedModel,\n315\t      valid_models: modelResolution.validModels,\n316\t    });\n317\t  }\n318\t  const model = modelResolution.model.id;\n319\t\n320\t  const domain = extractDomain(c);\n321\t  // Medical add-on only applies where the provider meters it (AssemblyAI today).\n322\t  const medical = domain === MEDICAL_DOMAIN;\n323\t\n324\t  const headerValidation = validateStreamingHeaders(c, provider);\n325\t  if (!headerValidation.ok) {\n326\t    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n327\t      reason: 'invalid_streaming_headers',\n328\t      flyRequestId,\n329\t      provider,\n330\t      status: headerValidation.response.status,\n331\t    });\n332\t    return headerValidation.response;\n333\t  }\n334\t\n335\t  const { contentType, contentLength } = headerValidation;\n336\t  // rawQuery, not c.req.query(): Hono's decoder adds an HTML-form `+` → space\n337\t  // step, corrupting values like a `C++` vocabulary term. See lib/query.ts.\n338\t  const language = rawQuery(c.req.url, 'language');\n339\t  const initialPrompt = rawQuery(c.req.url, 'initial_prompt');\n340\t  const mode = rawQuery(c.req.url, 'mode');\n341\t\n342\t  // ElevenLabs blocks API access from certain countries — the block surfaces\n343\t  // as a 200 OK with a text/html FAQ page (\"Do you restrict access ... for any\n344\t  // specific countries?\") instead of JSON. When the request lands on a Fly\n345\t  // machine in one of those countries, replay it via Fly's edge to `iad`\n346\t  // before doing any auth/credit work. Adds ~50-80ms vs ~6s of failure.\n347\t  // Verified blocked regions (2026-06-07): nrt (JP), bom (IN), maa (IN).\n348\t  //\n349\t  // Fly only honours `fly-replay` for request bodies ≤ 1 MB; larger requests\n350\t  // are silently executed in the original region. For oversized uploads from\n351\t  // a blocked region we skip the replay header and let the chain fall back\n352\t  // to the next provider instead of letting ElevenLabs return its HTML 200.\n353\t  let elevenlabsGeoBlocked = false;\n354\t  if (provider === 'elevenlabs' && ELEVENLABS_BLOCKED_FLY_REGIONS.has(process.env.FLY_REGION || '')) {\n355\t    if (contentLength <= FLY_REPLAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES) {\n356\t      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.fly_replay', {\n357\t        flyRequestId,\n358\t        provider,\n359\t        fromRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION,\n360\t        toRegion: const uploadMs = Math.round(performance.now() - uploadStart);\n433\t  const uploadBytesPerSec = uploadMs > 0\n434\t    ? Math.round((audioBuffer.byteLength / uploadMs) * 1000)\n435\t    : undefined;\n436\t  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.buffer_read_done', {\n437\t    audioBytes: audioBuffer.byteLength,\n438\t    uploadMs,\n439\t    uploadBytesPerSec,\n440\t  });\n441\t\n442\t  // The credit check above trusted the declared Content-Length. Received Content-Type: ${error.contentType}.`,\n596\t          {\n597\t            requestId,\n598\t            provider: current,\n599\t            received_content_type: error.contentType,\n600\t            accepted_formats: error.acceptedFormats,\n601\t          },\n602\t        );\n603\t      }\n604\t      // Non-retryable error (401 invalid key, etc.) — don't try fallbacks\n605\t      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_fail', {\n606\t        provider: current,\n607\t        attempt: index + 1,\n608\t        kind: 'non_retryable',\n609\t        message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n610\t      });\n611\t      return errorResponse(500, 'Transcription failed', error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error), { requestId });\n612\t    }\n613\t  }\n614\t\n615\t  // All providers in the chain failed.\n616\t  if (!result) {\n617\t    // Every provider rejected the input with a non-auth 4xx and none was merely\n618\t    // unavailable — the input itself is the problem, so a retry won't help.\n619\t    // Surface a 400 with the upstream message instead of a misleading 429/502\n620\t    // (\"rate-limited\"/\"unavailable\") that would have the client back off and\n621\t    // retry the same bad request. Surfacing a 429 implies \"we'll retry\n638\t    // through siblings, just back off\" — which is a lie when there are no\n639\t    // siblings. Return 502 with the upstream's actual error message so client\n640\t    // retry logic doesn't storm against a broken region.\n641\t    const isSelfOnlyChain = chain.length === 1;\n642\t    if (isSelfOnlyChain) {\n643\t      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_fail', {\n644\t        kind: 'self_only_chain_failed',\n645\t        provider,\n646\t        fallbackCount,\n647\t        attemptFailures,\n648\t        message: lastError?.message,\n649\t      });\n650\t      return errorResponse(502, `${PROVIDER_NAMES[provider]} unavailable`,\n651\t        lastError?.message ?? `${PROVIDER_NAMES[provider]} is currently unavailable. Please try again shortly.`,\n652\t        { requestId, provider },\n653\t      );\n654\t    }\n655\t\n656\t    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_fail', {\n657\t      kind: 'all_providers_unavailable',\n658\t      fallbackCount,\n659\t      attemptFailures,\n660\t      message: lastError?.message,\n661\t    });\n662\t    return errorResponse(429, 'All providers unavailable', 'All transcription providers are currently rate-limited. Please try again shortly.', { requestId });\n663\t  }\n664\t  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.stt_done', {\n665\t    provider: result.source,\n666\t    upstreamRequestId: result.requestId,\n667\t  });\n668\t\n669\t  const resultProvider: Provider = result.source === 'no_speech' ? provider : (result.source as Provider);\n670\t  const actualProvider = formatProviderName(resultProvider, usedModel);\n671\t  const providerName = fallbackFrom\n672\t    ? `${actualProvider} (fallback from ${formatProviderName(fallbackFrom, model)})`\n673\t    : actualProvider;\n674\t\n675\t  const noSpeech = result.source === 'no_speech';\n676\t  const creditsUsed = noSpeech ? 0 : creditsForCost(result.costUsd);\n677\t\n678\t  if (!noSpeech) {\n679\t    deductCredits(\n680\t      authResult.value,\n681\t      result.costUsd,\n682\t      {\n683\t        audio_duration_seconds: result.durationSeconds,\n684\t        transcription_cost_usd: result.costUsd,\n685\t        language: result.language ?? language ?? 'auto',\n686\t        mode,\n687\t        endpoint: '/transcribe',\n688\t        stt_provider: providerName,\n689\t        stt_model: usedModel || undefined,\n690\t      },\n691\t      clientIP\n692\t    ).catch(console.error);\n693\t  }\n694\t\n695\t  const response = {\n696\t    text: result.text,\n697\t    language: result.language,\n698\t    duration: result.durationSeconds,\n699\t    cost: {\n700\t      usd: result.costUsd,\n701\t      credits: creditsUsed,\n702\t    },\n703\t    metadata: {\n704\t      request_id: requestId,\n705\t      stt_provider: providerName,\n706\t      stt_model: usedModel || undefined,\n707\t    },\n708\t    ...(noSpeech ? { no_speech_detected: true } : {}),\n709\t  };\n710\t\n711\t  c.header('X-Request-ID', requestId);\n712\t  c.header('X-STT-Provider', providerName);\n713\t  if (usedModel) {\n714\t    c.header('X-STT-Model', usedModel);\n715\t  }\n716\t  c.header('X-Total-Cost-Usd', formatUsd(result.costUsd));\n717\t  c.header('X-Credits-Used', creditsUsed.toFixed(1));\n718\t\n719\t  const memUsageMb = Math.round(process.memoryUsage().rss / 1024 / 1024);\n720\t  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_done', {\n721\t    finalProvider: providerName,\n722\t    fallbackCount,\n723\t    // On a degraded success (fallbackCount > 0) this names which provider(s)\n724\t    // failed and why, so a slow-but-successful transcription is diagnosable\n725\t    // from the single outcome line.\n726\t    ...(attemptFailures.length ? { attemptFailures } : {}),\n727\t    noSpeech,\n728\t TRANSCRIPTION ROUTE\n// POST /transcribe - Main transcription endpoint\n// Supports multiple STT providers with automatic fallback\n\nimport type { Context } from 'hono';\nimport { transcribeWithDeepgram } from '../providers/deepgram';\nimport { transcribeWithGroq } from '../providers/groq';\nimport { transcribeWithElevenLabs } from '../providers/elevenlabs';\nimport { transcribeWithXaiGrok } from '../providers/xai-stt';\nimport { transcribeWithAzureMai } from '../providers/azure-mai';\nimport { transcribeWithGoogleChirp } from '../providers/google-chirp';\nimport { transcribeWithOpenAI } from '../providers/openai';\nimport { transcribeWithGemini } from '../providers/gemini';\nimport {\n  transcribeWithAssemblyAI,\n  hasExplicitLanguage as hasExplicitAssemblyAILanguage,\n  SYNC_ELIGIBLE_ESTIMATED_SECONDS as ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ELIGIBLE_ESTIMATED_SECONDS,\n} from '../providers/assemblyai';\nimport { transcribeWithMistral } from '../providers/mistral';\nimport { transcribeWithSoniox } from '../providers/soniox';\nimport type { ProviderRequestContext, TranscriptionResult } from '../providers/types';\nimport { AudioTooLargeError, ProviderInputError, ProviderUnavailableError, UnsupportedAudioFormatError } from '../providers/types';\nimport { creditsForCost, estimatePromptInputReservationUsd, formatUsd } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport {\n  estimatedUsdPerMinute,\n  getProviderDef,\n  isValidProviderId,\n  resolveModel,\n  MEDICAL_DOMAIN,\n  ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE,\n  type SttProviderId,\n} from '../lib/stt-models';\nimport { generateRequestId, getClientIP, getFlyRequestId } from '../lib/request-id';\nimport { rawQuery } from '../lib/query';\nimport {\n  FLY_REPLAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES,\n  GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES,\n  GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES,\n  MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES,\n  OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES,\n} from '../lib/constants';\nimport { isIPBlocked } from '../lib/redis';\nimport {\n  errorResponse,\n  fileTooLargeResponse,\n  invalidContentTypeResponse,\n  missingContentLengthResponse,\n} from '../lib/responses';\nimport { validateAuth } from '../middleware/auth';\nimport { deductCredits, estimateAudioSecondsFromSize, validateCredits } from '../middleware/credits';\nimport { flyProxyOverheadMs, logEvent, machineUptimeMs } from '../lib/logging';\n\n// Supported providers (mirror the server-side registry in lib/stt-models.ts).\nexport type Provider = SttProviderId;\n\n// Fly regions where ElevenLabs serves a text/html FAQ page instead of JSON\n// (geo-block on Japan + India confirmed via per-region smoke 2026-06-07).\n// Requests landing here are replayed to `iad` before any work happens.\nconst ELEVENLABS_BLOCKED_FLY_REGIONS = new Set(['nrt', 'bom', 'maa']);\nconst ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION = 'iad';\n\n// Human-readable base label per provider. The model is appended at runtime via\n// formatProviderName() so the response header / metering reflects exactly which\n// model ran (e.g. \"deepgram/nova-3-medical\", \"openai/gpt-4o-transcribe\").\nconst PROVIDER_NAMES: Record<Provider, string> = {\n  deepgram: 'deepgram',\n  elevenlabs: 'elevenlabs',\n  groq: 'groq',\n  grok: 'xai-grok',\n  'azure-mai': 'azure-mai',\n  'google-chirp': 'google-chirp',\n  openai: 'openai',\n  gemini: 'gemini',\n  assemblyai: 'assemblyai',\n  mistral: 'mistral',\n  soniox: 'soniox',\n};\n\nfunction formatProviderName(provider: Provider, model: string): string {\n  const base = PROVIDER_NAMES[provider];\n  return model ? `${base}/${model}` : base;\n}\n\n// Fallback chains: the original cheap trio (plus grok) cascade through\n// alternatives — ElevenLabs (most expensive) is the last resort. Every other\n// provider is SELF-ONLY: the caller picked that specific model, so on failure\n// we surface an error rather than silently substituting a different model and\n// price. (A cross-provider fallback would also change the metered cost.)\nconst FALLBACK_CHAINS: Record<Provider, Provider[]> = {\n  elevenlabs: ['elevenlabs', 'deepgram', 'groq'],\n  groq: ['groq', 'deepgram', 'elevenlabs'],\n  deepgram: ['deepgram', 'groq', 'elevenlabs'],\n  grok: ['grok', 'deepgram', 'groq', 'elevenlabs'],\n  'azure-mai': ['azure-mai'],\n  'google-chirp': ['google-chirp'],\n  openai: ['openai'],\n  gemini: ['gemini'],\n  assemblyai: ['assemblyai'],\n  mistral: ['mistral'],\n  soniox: ['soniox'],\n};\n\nconst PROVIDER_FN: Record<Provider, (\n  audio: ArrayBuffer,\n  contentType: string,\n  language?: string,\n  initialPrompt?: string,\n  context?: ProviderRequestContext,\n) => Promise<TranscriptionResult>> = {\n  deepgram: transcribeWithDeepgram,\n  groq: transcribeWithGroq,\n  elevenlabs: transcribeWithElevenLabs,\n  grok: transcribeWithXaiGrok,\n  'azure-mai': transcribeWithAzureMai,\n  'google-chirp': transcribeWithGoogleChirp,\n  openai: transcribeWithOpenAI,\n  gemini: transcribeWithGemini,\n  assemblyai: transcribeWithAssemblyAI,\n  mistral: transcribeWithMistral,\n  soniox: transcribeWithSoniox,\n};\n\n/**\n * Preflight credit reservation. For the primary provider we estimate against\n * the chosen model (and medical add-on); for fallback siblings we estimate\n * against their default model. The reservation uses the most expensive member\n * of the chain so we never under-reserve. `model`/`medical` are optional to\n * keep the historical 2-arg call signature working.\n */\nexport function estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks(\n  sizeBytes: number,\n  provider: Provider,\n  model?: string,\n  medical: boolean = false,\n  initialPrompt?: string,\n  language?: string,\n): number {\n  const chain = FALLBACK_CHAINS[provider];\n  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n  const hasInitialPrompt = Boolean(initialPrompt);\n  const rates = chain.map((p) => estimatedUsdPerMinute(\n    p,\n    p === provider ? model : undefined,\n    p === provider ? medical : false,\n    // The keyterm surcharge is billed by ANY chain member that supports it\n    // (ElevenLabs scribe_v2 / AssemblyAI universal-3-pro) whenever an\n    // initial_prompt is present — not just the primary provider. A\n    // Deepgram→ElevenLabs fallback still forwards initial_prompt and bills the\n    // +20% surcharge, so reserve for it on every eligible sibling. Other\n    // providers ignore the flag (estimatedUsdPerMinute scopes the add-on), so\n    // this never over-reserves for, say, a Deepgram-only success path.\n    hasInitialPrompt && (p === 'elevenlabs' || p === 'assemblyai'),\n  ));\n  // AssemblyAI's sync fast path (<120s, non-medical, EXPLICIT-language clips)\n  // always runs universal-3-5-pro at its OWN higher published rate — not the\n  // async catalog rate for the requested model (universal-2 / universal-3-pro),\n  // which `estimatedUsdPerMinute` above reserves against. A short clip is\n  // exactly sync's target case, so without this a short, non-medical\n  // AssemblyAI request could be deducted for more than was reserved. This\n  // condition must exactly mirror `transcribeWithAssemblyAI`'s real\n  // eligibility gate (medical + duration + explicit language) — reusing\n  // `hasExplicitLanguage` rather than reimplementing it here, since an\n  // auto-language request never actually routes through sync (sync has no\n  // auto-detect) and over-reserving for it could wrongly reject a low-balance\n  // user at preflight for a request that will only ever go through the\n  // cheaper async path.\n  if (\n    provider === 'assemblyai' && !medical\n    && estimatedSeconds < ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ELIGIBLE_ESTIMATED_SECONDS\n    && hasExplicitAssemblyAILanguage(language)\n  ) {\n    rates.push(ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE);\n  }\n  const usdPerMinute = Math.max(...rates);\n  // Token-billed providers (Gemini, OpenAI gpt-4o*) charge the prompt text as\n  // input tokens on top of the audio. Reserve that flat cost for the primary\n  // provider (these are self-only chains) so a large vocabulary prompt on a\n  // short clip can't be deducted beyond what was reserved.\n  const promptReservationUsd = estimatePromptInputReservationUsd(provider, model, initialPrompt);\n  const estimatedCostUsd = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * usdPerMinute + promptReservationUsd;\n  return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(estimatedCostUsd));\n}\n\ntype ProviderSelection =\n  | { ok: true; provider: Provider }\n  | { ok: false; provided: string };\n\nfunction extractProvider(c: Context): ProviderSelection {\n  const header = c.req.header('X-STT-Provider')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n  // No header → historical default (many clients send only a provider, some\n  // none). An explicitly-supplied but unknown provider is REJECTED (fail-closed)\n  // rather than silently billed against a default upstream.\n  if (!header) {\n    return { ok: true, provider: 'deepgram' };\n  }\n  if (isValidProviderId(header)) {\n    return { ok: true, provider: header };\n  }\n  return { ok: false, provided: header };\n}\n\nfunction extractModel(c: Context): string | undefined {\n  return c.req.header('X-STT-Model')?.trim() || rawQuery(c.req.url, 'model')?.trim() || undefined;\n}\n\nfunction extractDomain(c: Context): string | undefined {\n  const domain = c.req.header('X-STT-Domain')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n  return domain || undefined;\n}\n\nfunction validateStreamingHeaders(c: Context, provider: Provider):\n  | { ok: true; contentType: string; contentLength: number }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response } {\n  const contentType = c.req.header('Content-Type') || '';\n  if (!contentType.startsWith('audio/')) {\n    return { ok: false, response: invalidContentTypeResponse('audio/*', contentType) };\n  }\n\n  const contentLengthHeader = c.req.header('Content-Length');\n  if (!contentLengthHeader) {\n    return { ok: false, response: missingContentLengthResponse() };\n  }\n\n  const contentLength = Number.parseInt(contentLengthHeader, 10);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(contentLength) || contentLength <= 0) {\n    return { ok: false, response: errorResponse(400, 'Invalid Content-Length', 'Content-Length must be a positive integer') };\n  }\n\n  if (contentLength > MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES) {\n    return { ok: false, response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES) };\n  }\n\n  // Google Chirp inline cap (~9.5 MB) applies before we buffer the body —\n  // without a scratch GCS bucket the provider has no path for larger audio,\n  // and we don't want to allocate a 50 MB buffer just to 413 the caller.\n  if (\n    provider === 'google-chirp'\n    && contentLength > GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES\n    && !(process.env.GOOGLE_SPEECH_GCS_BUCKET || '').trim()\n  ) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Gemini sends audio inline (base64) and rejects anything over ~14 MB raw.\n  // Gate on Content-Length before buffering so an oversized upload is rejected\n  // early instead of after buffering up to MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES on the machine.\n  if (provider === 'gemini' && contentLength > GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n    };\n  }\n\n  // OpenAI hard-rejects audio over 25 MB with a 400. Gate on Content-Length\n  // before buffering so we return 413 without allocating the buffer first.\n  if (provider === 'openai' && contentLength > OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n    };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true, contentType, contentLength };\n}\n\nexport async function transcribeRoute(c: Context) {\n  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n  const startTime = performance.now();\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n  const flyRequestId = getFlyRequestId(c);\n\n  // IP block check\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'ip_blocked',\n      flyRequestId,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(403, 'Access denied', 'Your IP has been temporarily blocked due to abuse');\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.ip_check_done', { flyRequestId });\n\n  const providerSelection = extractProvider(c);\n  if (!providerSelection.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'invalid_provider',\n      flyRequestId,\n      provided: providerSelection.provided,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid STT provider',\n      `Unknown X-STT-Provider \"${providerSelection.provided}\".`,\n      { requestId, provided: providerSelection.provided },\n    );\n  }\n  const provider = providerSelection.provider;\n\n  // Resolve + validate the requested model against the server-side registry.\n  // An unknown model for the provider is rejected (fail-closed) rather than\n  // silently routed to the provider default at a possibly different price.\n  const requestedModel = extractModel(c);\n  const modelResolution = resolveModel(provider, requestedModel);\n  if (!modelResolution.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'invalid_model',\n      flyRequestId,\n      provider,\n      requestedModel,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid STT model', modelResolution.reason, {\n      requestId,\n      provider,\n      requested_model: requestedModel,\n      valid_models: modelResolution.validModels,\n    });\n  }\n  const model = modelResolution.model.id;\n\n  const domain = extractDomain(c);\n  // Medical add-on only applies where the provider meters it (AssemblyAI today).\n  const medical = domain === MEDICAL_DOMAIN;\n\n  const headerValidation = validateStreamingHeaders(c, provider);\n  if (!headerValidation.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'invalid_streaming_headers',\n      flyRequestId,\n      provider,\n      status: headerValidation.response.status,\n    });\n    return headerValidation.response;\n  }\n\n  const { contentType, contentLength } = headerValidation;\n  // rawQuery, not c.req.query(): Hono's decoder adds an HTML-form `+` → space\n  // step, corrupting values like a `C++` vocabulary term. See lib/query.ts.\n  const language = rawQuery(c.req.url, 'language');\n  const initialPrompt = rawQuery(c.req.url, 'initial_prompt');\n  const mode = rawQuery(c.req.url, 'mode');\n\n  // ElevenLabs blocks API access from certain countries — the block surfaces\n  // as a 200 OK with a text/html FAQ page (\"Do you restrict access ... for any\n  // specific countries?\") instead of JSON. When the request lands on a Fly\n  // machine in one of those countries, replay it via Fly's edge to `iad`\n  // before doing any auth/credit work. Adds ~50-80ms vs ~6s of failure.\n  // Verified blocked regions (2026-06-07): nrt (JP), bom (IN), maa (IN).\n  //\n  // Fly only honours `fly-replay` for request bodies ≤ 1 MB; larger requests\n  // are silently executed in the original region. For oversized uploads from\n  // a blocked region we skip the replay header and let the chain fall back\n  // to the next provider instead of letting ElevenLabs return its HTML 200.\n  let elevenlabsGeoBlocked = false;\n  if (provider === 'elevenlabs' && ELEVENLABS_BLOCKED_FLY_REGIONS.has(process.env.FLY_REGION || '')) {\n    if (contentLength <= FLY_REPLAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES) {\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.fly_replay', {\n        flyRequestId,\n        provider,\n        fromRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION,\n        toRegion: ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION,\n        reason: 'elevenlabs_geo_block',\n      });\n      c.header('fly-replay', `region=${ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION}`);\n      return c.body(null, 200);\n    }\n\n    elevenlabsGeoBlocked = true;\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.fly_replay_skipped_oversized', {\n      flyRequestId,\n      provider,\n      flyRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION,\n      contentLength,\n      replayMaxBytes: FLY_REPLAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const proxyOverheadMs = flyProxyOverheadMs(c.req.header('Fly-Request-Start'));\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_start', {\n    flyRequestId,\n    flyRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n    flyMachineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID,\n    proxyOverheadMs,\n    provider,\n    model: model || 'default',\n    domain: domain || 'none',\n    contentType,\n    contentLength,\n    language: language || 'auto',\n    hasInitialPrompt: Boolean(initialPrompt),\n    mode: mode || 'default',\n  });\n\n  // Auth (query params only) — Cloud is licensed-only; a valid account key is required.\n  // `account_key` is the canonical param name; `license_key` is the legacy alias\n  // that installed native apps still send, so we accept either.\n  const authResult = await validateAuth({\n    licenseKey:\n      rawQuery(c.req.url, 'account_key') ?? rawQuery(c.req.url, 'license_key'),\n  });\n  if (!authResult.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'auth_failed',\n      flyRequestId,\n      status: authResult.response.status,\n    });\n    return authResult.response;\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.auth_done');\n\n  // Vocabulary surcharge: AssemblyAI charges a keyterms_prompt add-on (universal-3-pro)\n  // and ElevenLabs a +20% keyterm surcharge (scribe_v2) when an initial_prompt is supplied.\n  // We pass the raw hasInitialPrompt flag through to the reservation so it can reserve the\n  // surcharge for ANY eligible chain member — including ElevenLabs reached via a\n  // Deepgram/Groq/Grok fallback, which still forwards the prompt and bills the surcharge.\n  // estimatedUsdPerMinute scopes the add-on to universal-3-pro / scribe_v2, so passing it\n  // for every request is safe and never under-reserves.\n  const estimatedCredits = estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks(contentLength, provider, model, medical, initialPrompt, language);\n  const creditCheck = await validateCredits(authResult.value, estimatedCredits, clientIP);\n  if (!creditCheck.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'credits_failed',\n      flyRequestId,\n      status: creditCheck.response.status,\n      estimatedCredits,\n    });\n    return creditCheck.response;\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.credits_done', { estimatedCredits });\n\n  const uploadStart = performance.now();\n  const audioBuffer = await c.req.arrayBuffer();\n  const uploadMs = Math.round(performance.now() - uploadStart);\n  const uploadBytesPerSec = uploadMs > 0\n    ? Math.round((audioBuffer.byteLength / uploadMs) * 1000)\n    : undefined;\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.buffer_read_done', {\n    audioBytes: audioBuffer.byteLength,\n    uploadMs,\n    uploadBytesPerSec,\n  });\n\n  // The credit check above trusted the declared Content-Length. Reject bodies\n  // that arrive larger than declared so a client can't under-declare to pass\n  // validateCredits cheaply and then stream a bigger payload we'd pay the\n  // provider for (issue ray-amjad/hyperwhisper#263). Honest clients always\n  // send a body that matches Content-Length exactly.\n  if (audioBuffer.byteLength > contentLength) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'content_length_mismatch',\n      flyRequestId,\n      declaredBytes: contentLength,\n      actualBytes: audioBuffer.byteLength,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Content-Length mismatch',\n      `Request body (${audioBuffer.byteLength} bytes) exceeds the declared Content-Length (${contentLength} bytes)`,\n      { requestId, declared_bytes: contentLength, actual_bytes: audioBuffer.byteLength },\n    );\n  }\n\n  let result: TranscriptionResult | undefined;\n  let fallbackFrom: Provider | undefined;\n  let fallbackCount = 0;\n  // The model that actually produced the result. Defaults to the requested\n  // model; on a cross-provider fallback it becomes that sibling's default model.\n  let usedModel = model;\n\n  // When the request landed in a region where ElevenLabs is geo-blocked AND\n  // the payload was too large to fly-replay, drop ElevenLabs from the chain\n  // so we fall through to the next provider instead of failing the chain on\n  // ElevenLabs's HTML-200 geo-block response.\n  const chain = elevenlabsGeoBlocked\n    ? FALLBACK_CHAINS[provider].filter(p => p !== 'elevenlabs')\n    : FALLBACK_CHAINS[provider];\n  let lastError: Error | undefined;\n  let lastInputError: ProviderInputError | undefined;\n  let sawUnavailable = false;\n  // Per-attempt failure breadcrumbs, surfaced on the final outcome log so one\n  // line explains a degraded/failed request (which provider failed, why, how\n  // long it hung) without correlating separate provider-level log events.\n  const attemptFailures: Array<{\n    provider: Provider;\n    kind: string;\n    status?: number;\n    attemptMs?: number;\n  }> = [];\n\n  for (const [index, current] of chain.entries()) {\n    // The chosen model + domain only apply to the provider the caller picked.\n    // Fallback siblings run their own default model (the caller's model id is\n    // meaningless to them) and never inherit the medical add-on.\n    const attemptModel = current === provider ? model : getProviderDef(current).defaultModel;\n    const attemptDomain = current === provider ? domain : undefined;\n\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.provider_attempt_start', {\n      provider: current,\n      model: attemptModel || 'default',\n      attempt: index + 1,\n    });\n\n    try {\n      result = await PROVIDER_FN[current](audioBuffer, contentType, language, initialPrompt, {\n        requestId,\n        attempt: index + 1,\n        model: attemptModel,\n        domain: attemptDomain,\n      });\n      // Prefer the model the adapter reports it ACTUALLY ran (e.g. AssemblyAI's\n      // universal-3-pro → universal-2 fallback for unsupported languages) so the\n      // X-STT-Model header and deduction metadata match what was billed; fall\n      // back to the attempted model when the adapter doesn't report one.\n      usedModel = result.model || attemptModel;\n      if (current !== provider) {\n        fallbackFrom = provider;\n      }\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.provider_attempt_done', {\n        provider: current,\n        model: attemptModel || 'default',\n        attempt: index + 1,\n        upstreamRequestId: result.requestId,\n        transcriptChars: result.text.length,\n        resultSource: result.source,\n      });\n      break;\n    } catch (error) {\n      if (error instanceof ProviderUnavailableError) {\n        const next = chain[chain.indexOf(current) + 1];\n        fallbackCount += 1;\n        // `unavailableKind` distinguishes the root cause inline — `timeout`\n        // (we gave up; upstream may have been fine) vs `upstream_5xx` /\n        // `rate_limit` (upstream actually failed) vs `bad_response` (geo-block\n        // HTML / empty body) — instead of the old catch-all `provider_unavailable`.\n        logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.provider_attempt_fail', {\n          provider: current,\n          attempt: index + 1,\n          kind: 'provider_unavailable',\n          unavailableKind: error.kind,\n          upstreamStatus: error.status,\n          attemptMs: error.elapsedMs,\n          message: error.message,\n          nextProvider: next,\n        });\n        attemptFailures.push({\n          provider: current,\n          kind: error.kind,\n          status: error.status,\n          attemptMs: error.elapsedMs,\n        });\n        lastError = error;\n        sawUnavailable = true;\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (error instanceof ProviderInputError) {\n        // The provider rejected this specific input (e.g. ElevenLabs 400 on a\n        // language code it doesn't accept). A sibling provider may accept the\n        // same input, so continue the fallback chain instead of failing the\n        // whole request. (issue ray-amjad/hyperwhisper#333)\n        const next = chain[chain.indexOf(current) + 1];\n        fallbackCount += 1;\n        logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.provider_attempt_fail', {\n          provider: current,\n          attempt: index + 1,\n          kind: 'provider_input_rejected',\n          status: error.status,\n          message: error.message,\n          nextProvider: next,\n        });\n        lastError = error;\n        lastInputError = error;\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (error instanceof AudioTooLargeError) {\n        logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_fail', {\n          provider: current,\n          attempt: index + 1,\n          kind: 'audio_too_large',\n          message: error.message,\n          actualBytes: error.actualBytes,\n          maxBytes: error.maxBytes,\n        });\n        return errorResponse(413, 'Audio too large for provider',\n          `${PROVIDER_NAMES[current]} accepts at most ${Math.round(error.maxBytes / (1024 * 1024))} MB inline. Your audio is ${(error.actualBytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)} MB.`,\n          { requestId, provider: current, max_size_mb: Math.round(error.maxBytes / (1024 * 1024)), actual_size_mb: parseFloat((error.actualBytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2)) },\n        );\n      }\n      if (error instanceof UnsupportedAudioFormatError) {\n        logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_fail', {\n          provider: current,\n          attempt: index + 1,\n          kind: 'unsupported_audio_format',\n          message: error.message,\n          receivedContentType: error.contentType,\n          acceptedFormats: error.acceptedFormats,\n        });\n        return errorResponse(415, 'Unsupported audio format for provider',\n          `${PROVIDER_NAMES[current]} accepts only ${error.acceptedFormats.join(', ')}. Received Content-Type: ${error.contentType}.`,\n          {\n            requestId,\n            provider: current,\n            received_content_type: error.contentType,\n            accepted_formats: error.acceptedFormats,\n          },\n        );\n      }\n      // Non-retryable error (401 invalid key, etc.) — don't try fallbacks\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_fail', {\n        provider: current,\n        attempt: index + 1,\n        kind: 'non_retryable',\n        message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n      });\n      return errorResponse(500, 'Transcription failed', error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error), { requestId });\n    }\n  }\n\n  // All providers in the chain failed.\n  if (!result) {\n    // Every provider rejected the input with a non-auth 4xx and none was merely\n    // unavailable — the input itself is the problem, so a retry won't help.\n    // Surface a 400 with the upstream message instead of a misleading 429/502\n    // (\"rate-limited\"/\"unavailable\") that would have the client back off and\n    // retry the same bad request. (issue ray-amjad/hyperwhisper#333)\n    if (lastInputError && !sawUnavailable) {\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_fail', {\n        kind: 'all_providers_rejected_input',\n        provider,\n        fallbackCount,\n        status: lastInputError.status,\n        message: lastInputError.message,\n      });\n      return errorResponse(400, 'Transcription input rejected',\n        `No transcription provider accepted this request: ${lastInputError.message}`,\n        { requestId, provider },\n      );\n    }\n\n    // Self-only chains (e.g. azure-mai, google-chirp) mean the user explicitly\n    // opted into a single upstream. Surfacing a 429 implies \"we'll retry\n    // through siblings, just back off\" — which is a lie when there are no\n    // siblings. Return 502 with the upstream's actual error message so client\n    // retry logic doesn't storm against a broken region.\n    const isSelfOnlyChain = chain.length === 1;\n    if (isSelfOnlyChain) {\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_fail', {\n        kind: 'self_only_chain_failed',\n        provider,\n        fallbackCount,\n        attemptFailures,\n        message: lastError?.message,\n      });\n      return errorResponse(502, `${PROVIDER_NAMES[provider]} unavailable`,\n        lastError?.message ?? `${PROVIDER_NAMES[provider]} is currently unavailable. Please try again shortly.`,\n        { requestId, provider },\n      );\n    }\n\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_fail', {\n      kind: 'all_providers_unavailable',\n      fallbackCount,\n      attemptFailures,\n      message: lastError?.message,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(429, 'All providers unavailable', 'All transcription providers are currently rate-limited. Please try again shortly.', { requestId });\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.stt_done', {\n    provider: result.source,\n    upstreamRequestId: result.requestId,\n  });\n\n  const resultProvider: Provider = result.source === 'no_speech' ? provider : (result.source as Provider);\n  const actualProvider = formatProviderName(resultProvider, usedModel);\n  const providerName = fallbackFrom\n    ? `${actualProvider} (fallback from ${formatProviderName(fallbackFrom, model)})`\n    : actualProvider;\n\n  const noSpeech = result.source === 'no_speech';\n  const creditsUsed = noSpeech ? 0 : creditsForCost(result.costUsd);\n\n  if (!noSpeech) {\n    deductCredits(\n      authResult.value,\n      result.costUsd,\n      {\n        audio_duration_seconds: result.durationSeconds,\n        transcription_cost_usd: result.costUsd,\n        language: result.language ?? language ?? 'auto',\n        mode,\n        endpoint: '/transcribe',\n        stt_provider: providerName,\n        stt_model: usedModel || undefined,\n      },\n      clientIP\n    ).catch(console.error);\n  }\n\n  const response = {\n    text: result.text,\n    language: result.language,\n    duration: result.durationSeconds,\n    cost: {\n      usd: result.costUsd,\n      credits: creditsUsed,\n    },\n    metadata: {\n      request_id: requestId,\n      stt_provider: providerName,\n      stt_model: usedModel || undefined,\n    },\n    ...(noSpeech ? { no_speech_detected: true } : {}),\n  };\n\n  c.header('X-Request-ID', requestId);\n  c.header('X-STT-Provider', providerName);\n  if (usedModel) {\n    c.header('X-STT-Model', usedModel);\n  }\n  c.header('X-Total-Cost-Usd', formatUsd(result.costUsd));\n  c.header('X-Credits-Used', creditsUsed.toFixed(1));\n\n  const memUsageMb = Math.round(process.memoryUsage().rss / 1024 / 1024);\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_done', {\n    finalProvider: providerName,\n    fallbackCount,\n    // On a degraded success (fallbackCount > 0) this names which provider(s)\n    // failed and why, so a slow-but-successful transcription is diagnosable\n    // from the single outcome line.\n    ...(attemptFailures.length ? { attemptFailures } : {}),\n    noSpeech,\n    creditsUsed,\n    flyMachineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID,\n    machineUptimeMs: machineUptimeMs(),\n    rssMb: memUsageMb,\n  });\n  return c.json(response);\n}\n","numLines":735,"startLine":1,"totalLines":735}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"47ee14f0-fb3e-4386-8a02-a4e88c353758","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"a86b3d36-348d-415c-add3-88a2d20876c5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a1f765d637aca55e2","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KCsmjf68h8PcUf9kSS4dtJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// LLM PROVIDER SELECTION + RETRY\n2\t\n3\timport { retryWithBackoff } from './utils';\n4\timport type { CorrectionRequestPayload } from '../providers/groq-llm';\n5\timport { requestCerebrasChat } from '../providers/cerebras';\n6\timport { requestGroqChat } from '../providers/groq-llm';\n7\timport { requestAnthropicChat } from '../providers/anthropic';\n8\timport { requestXaiGrokChat } from '../providers/xai-llm';\n9\timport { requestOpenAIChat } from '../providers/openai-llm';\n10\timport { requestGeminiChat } from '../providers/gemini-llm';\n11\timport { requestMistralChat } from '../providers/mistral-llm';\n12\t\n13\texport type LLMProvider = 'cerebras' | 'groq' | 'anthropic' | 'grok' | 'openai' | 'gemini' | 'mistral';\n14\t\n15\texport const DEFAULT_LLM_PROVIDER: LLMProvider = 'cerebras';\n16\t\n17\texport const LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES: Record<LLMProvider, string> = {\n18\t  cerebras: 'cerebras-gpt-oss-120b',\n19\t  groq: 'groq-gpt-oss-120b',\n20\t  anthropic: 'claude-haiku-4-5',\n21\t  grok: 'xai-grok-4.3',\n22\t  openai: 'openai-gpt-5-mini',\n23\t  gemini: 'gemini-2.5-flash',\n24\t  mistral: 'mistral-small-latest',\n25\t};\n26\t\n27\t// Served name per (provider, resolved-model) pair, for the X-LLM-Provider\n28\t// response header / log. The default model maps back to LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES so\n29\t// single-model providers (and the default of the multi-model ones) are\n30\t// unchanged; the non-default allowlisted models of the multi-model providers\n31\t// (openai/gemini/mistral) get their own label so the response reflects the model\n32\t// actually used instead of the provider default. MUST stay in sync with\n33\t// LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS allowlists.\n34\tconst LLM_SERVED_NAMES: Partial<Record<LLMProvider, Record<string, string>>> = {\n35\t  openai: { 'gpt-5-mini': 'openai-gpt-5-mini', 'gpt-5-nano': 'openai-gpt-5-nano' },\n36\t  gemini: { 'gemini-2.5-flash': 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite' },\n37\t  mistral: { 'mistral-small-latest': 'mistral-small-latest', 'open-mistral-nemo': 'open-mistral-nemo' },\n38\t};\n39\t\n40\t/**\n41\t * Served name for the X-LLM-Provider response header / log: the (provider,\n42\t * model) pair the request was actually answered with. For the single-model\n43\t * providers (and any model not in the served-name map) this is the static\n44\t * LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES label; for the multi-model providers' non-default models it\n45\t * echoes the resolved model so callers (and the smoke test) can tell a\n46\t * non-default model apart from the provider default.\n47\t */\n48\texport function servedLLMName(provider: LLMProvider, model: string): string {\n49\t  return LLM_SERVED_NAMES[provider]?.[model] ?? LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES[provider];\n50\t}\n51\t\n52\tconst LLM_PROVIDER_FALLBACKS: Record<LLMProvider, LLMProvider> = {\n53\t  anthropic: 'cerebras',\n54\t  cerebras: 'groq',\n55\t  groq: 'cerebras',\n56\t  grok: 'anthropic',\n57\t  openai: 'anthropic',\n58\t  gemini: 'cerebras',\n59\t  mistral: 'groq',\n60\t};\n61\t\n62\t// Per-provider allowlist of valid X-LLM-Model ids, with the default first. The\n63\t// resolved model is threaded through callWithRetry to the openai/gemini/mistral\n64\t// clients (the 4 single-model providers ignore it). MUST match the model ids in\n65\t// shared-app-classification/cloud-pp-catalog.json.\n66\tconst LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS: Record<LLMProvider, { default: string; allowed: readonly string[] }> = {\n67\t  cerebras: { default: 'gpt-oss-120b', allowed: ['gpt-oss-120b'] },\n68\t  groq: { default: 'openai/gpt-oss-120b', allowed: ['openai/gpt-oss-120b'] },\n69\t  anthropic: { default: 'claude-haiku-4-5', allowed: ['claude-haiku-4-5'] },\n70\t  grok: { default: 'grok-4.3', allowed: ['grok-4.3'] },\n71\t  openai: { default: 'gpt-5-mini', allowed: ['gpt-5-mini', 'gpt-5-nano'] },\n72\t  gemini: { default: 'gemini-2.5-flash', allowed: ['gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'] },\n73\t  mistral: { default: 'mistral-small-latest', allowed: ['mistral-small-latest', 'open-mistral-nemo'] },\n74\t};\n75\t\n76\texport function defaultModelFor(provider: LLMProvider): string {\n77\t  return LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider].default;\n78\t}\n79\t\n80\texport function fallbackProviderFor(provider: LLMProvider): LLMProvider {\n81\t  return LLM_PROVIDER_FALLBACKS[provider];\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\t/**\n85\t * Extract LLM provider from X-LLM-Provider header.\n86\t * Returns default provider if header is missing or invalid.\n87\t */\n88\texport function extractLLMProvider(request: Request): LLMProvider {\n89\t  const header = request.headers.get('x-llm-provider')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n90\t\n91\t  switch (header) {\n92\t    case 'groq':\n93\t    case 'cerebras':\n94\t    case 'anthropic':\n95\t    case 'grok':\n96\t    case 'openai':\n97\t    case 'gemini':\n98\t    case 'mistral':\n99\t      return header;\n100\t    default:\n101\t      return DEFAULT_LLM_PROVIDER;\n102\t  }\n103\t}\n104\t\n105\t/**\n106\t * Resolve the model for a provider from the X-LLM-Model header, validating it\n107\t * against the provider's allowlist. Missing or invalid models fall back to the\n108\t * provider default so a bad header never bills the wrong (or no) model.\n109\t */\n110\texport function resolveLLMModel(provider: LLMProvider, request: Request): string {\n111\t  const requested = request.headers.get('x-llm-model')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n112\t  const config = LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider];\n113\t  if (requested && config.allowed.includes(requested)) {\n114\t    return requested;\n115\t  }\n116\t  return config.default;\n117\t}\n118\t\n119\t/**\n120\t * Retry LLM call with exponential backoff. `model` is the resolved (allowlisted)\n121\t * model id — the multi-model providers (openai/gemini/mistral) route on it; the\n122\t * single-model providers ignore it.\n123\t */\n124\texport async function callWithRetry(\n125\t  provider: LLMProvider,\n126\t  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n127\t  requestId: string,\n128\t  maxRetries: number,\n129\t  model: string\n130\t): Promise<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof requestCerebrasChat>>> {\n131\t  return retryWithBackoff(\n132\t    () => {\n133\t      if (provider === 'anthropic') return requestAnthropicChat(payload, requestId);\n134\t      if (provider === 'grok') return requestXaiGrokChat(payload, requestId);\n135\t      if (provider === 'openai') return requestOpenAIChat(payload, requestId, model);\n136\t      if (provider === 'gemini') return requestGeminiChat(payload, requestId, model);\n137\t      if (provider === 'mistral') return requestMistralChat(payload, requestId, model);\n138\t      return provider === 'cerebras'\n139\t        ? requestCerebrasChat(payload, requestId)\n140\t        : requestGroqChat(payload, requestId);\n141\t    },\n142\t    {\n143\t      maxRetries,\n144\t      initialDelayMs: 1000,\n145\t      backoffMultiplier: 2,\n146\t      onRetry: (attempt, error, delayMs) => {\n147\t        console.warn(`[llm] ${provider} failed - retrying`, {\n148\t          attempt,\n149\t          error: error.message,\n150\t          delayMs,\n151\t        });\n152\t      },\n153\t    }\n154\t  );\n155\t}\n156\t\n157\tfunction getErrorStatus(error: unknown): number | undefined {\n158\t  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') {\n159\t    return undefined;\n160\t  }\n161\t\n162\t  const status = (error as { status?: unknown }).status;\n163\t  if (typeof status === 'number') {\n164\t    return status;\n165\t  }\n166\t\n167\t  const message = (error as { message?: unknown }).message;\n168\t  if (typeof message === 'string') {\n169\t    const match = message.match(/status\\s+(\\d{3})/i);\n170\t    if (match) {\n171\t      return Number(match[1]);\n172\t    }\n173\t  }\n174\t\n175\t  return undefined;\n176\t}\n177\t\n178\t/**\n179\t * Check if an error should trigger provider fallback (5xx).\n180\t */\n181\texport function shouldFallback(error: unknown): boolean {\n182\t  const status = getErrorStatus(error);\n183\t  return typeof status === 'number' && status >= 500 && status <= 599;\n184\t}\n185\t"}]},"uuid":"2fb949e2-897b-4efe-9d75-0dda3d9ce117","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:44:52.666Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/llm-provider.ts","content":"// '../providers/cerebras';\nimport { requestGroqChat } from '../providers/groq-llm';\nimport { requestAnthropicChat } from '../providers/anthropic';\nimport { requestXaiGrokChat } from '../providers/xai-llm';\nimport { requestOpenAIChat } from '../providers/openai-llm';\nimport { requestGeminiChat } from '../providers/gemini-llm';\nimport { requestMistralChat } from '../providers/mistral-llm';\n\nexport type LLMProvider = 'cerebras' | 'groq' | 'anthropic' | 'grok' | 'openai' | 'gemini' | 'mistral';\n\nexport const DEFAULT_LLM_PROVIDER: LLMProvider = 'cerebras';\n\nexport const LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES: Record<LLMProvider, string> = {\n  cerebras: 'cerebras-gpt-oss-120b',\n  groq: 'groq-gpt-oss-120b',\n  anthropic: 'claude-haiku-4-5',\n  grok: 'xai-grok-4.3',\n  openai: 'openai-gpt-5-mini',\n  gemini: 'gemini-2.5-flash',\n  mistral: 'mistral-small-latest',\n};\n\n// Served name per (provider, resolved-model) pair, for the X-LLM-Provider\n// response header / log. The default model maps back to LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES so\n// single-model providers (and the default of the multi-model ones) are\n// unchanged; the non-default allowlisted models of the multi-model providers\n// (openai/gemini/mistral) get their own label so the response reflects the model\n// actually used instead of the provider default. MUST stay in sync with\n// LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS allowlists.\nconst LLM_SERVED_NAMES: Partial<Record<LLMProvider, Record<string, string>>> = {\n  openai: { 'gpt-5-mini': 'openai-gpt-5-mini', 'gpt-5-nano': 'openai-gpt-5-nano' },\n  gemini: { 'gemini-2.5-flash': 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite' },\n  mistral: { 'mistral-small-latest': 'mistral-small-latest', 'open-mistral-nemo': 'open-mistral-nemo' },\n};\n\n/**\n * Served name for the X-LLM-Provider response header / log: the (provider,\n * model) pair the request was actually answered with. For the single-model\n * providers (and any model not in the served-name map) this is the static\n * LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES label; for the multi-model providers' non-default models it\n * echoes the resolved model so callers (and the smoke test) can tell a\n * non-default model apart from the provider default.\n */\nexport function servedLLMName(provider: LLMProvider, model: string): string {\n  return LLM_SERVED_NAMES[provider]?.[model] ?? LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES[provider];\n}\n\nconst LLM_PROVIDER_FALLBACKS: Record<LLMProvider, LLMProvider> = {\n  anthropic: 'cerebras',\n  cerebras: 'groq',\n  groq: 'cerebras',\n  grok: 'anthropic',\n  openai: 'anthropic',\n  gemini: 'cerebras',\n  mistral: 'groq',\n};\n\n// Per-provider allowlist of valid X-LLM-Model ids, with the default first. The\n// resolved model is threaded through callWithRetry to the openai/gemini/mistral\n// clients (the 4 single-model providers ignore it). MUST match the model ids in\n// shared-app-classification/cloud-pp-catalog.json.\nconst LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS: Record<LLMProvider, { default: string; allowed: readonly string[] }> = {\n  cerebras: { default: 'gpt-oss-120b', allowed: ['gpt-oss-120b'] },\n  groq: { default: 'openai/gpt-oss-120b', allowed: ['openai/gpt-oss-120b'] },\n  anthropic: { default: 'claude-haiku-4-5', allowed: ['claude-haiku-4-5'] },\n  grok: { default: 'grok-4.3', allowed: ['grok-4.3'] },\n  openai: { default: 'gpt-5-mini', allowed: ['gpt-5-mini', 'gpt-5-nano'] },\n  gemini: { default: 'gemini-2.5-flash', allowed: ['gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'] },\n  mistral: { default: 'mistral-small-latest', allowed: ['mistral-small-latest', 'open-mistral-nemo'] },\n};\n\nexport function defaultModelFor(provider: LLMProvider): string {\n  return LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider].default;\n}\n\nexport function fallbackProviderFor(provider: LLMProvider): LLMProvider {\n  return LLM_PROVIDER_FALLBACKS[provider];\n}\n\n/**\n * Extract LLM provider from X-LLM-Provider header.\n * Returns default provider if header is missing or invalid.\n */\nexport function extractLLMProvider(request: Request): LLMProvider {\n  const header = request.headers.get('x-llm-provider')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n\n  switch (header) {\n    case 'groq':\n    case 'cerebras':\n    case 'anthropic':\n    case 'grok':\n    case 'openai':\n    case 'gemini':\n    case 'mistral':\n      return header;\n    default:\n      return DEFAULT_LLM_PROVIDER;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve the model for a provider from the X-LLM-Model header, validating it\n * against the provider's allowlist. Missing or invalid models fall back to the\n * provider default so a bad header never bills the wrong (or no) model.\n */\nexport function resolveLLMModel(provider: LLMProvider, request: Request): string {\n  const requested = request.headers.get('x-llm-model')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n  const config = LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider];\n  if (requested && config.allowed.includes(requested)) {\n    return requested;\n  }\n  return config.default;\n}\n\n/**\n * Retry LLM call with exponential backoff. `model` is the resolved (allowlisted)\n * model id — the multi-model providers (openai/gemini/mistral) route on it; the\n * single-model providers ignore it.\n */\nexport async function callWithRetry(\n  provider: LLMProvider,\n  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n  requestId: string,\n  maxRetries: number,\n  model: string\n): Promise<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof requestCerebrasChat>>> {\n  return retryWithBackoff(\n    () => {\n      if (provider === 'anthropic') return requestAnthropicChat(payload, requestId);\n      if (provider === 'grok') return requestXaiGrokChat(payload, requestId);\n      if (provider === 'openai') return requestOpenAIChat(payload, requestId, model);\n      if (provider === 'gemini') return requestGeminiChat(payload, requestId, model);\n      if (provider === 'mistral') return requestMistralChat(payload, requestId, model);\n      return provider === 'cerebras'\n        ? requestCerebrasChat(payload, requestId)\n        : requestGroqChat(payload, requestId);\n    },\n    {\n      maxRetries,\n      initialDelayMs: 1000,\n      backoffMultiplier: 2,\n      onRetry: (attempt, error, delayMs) => {\n        console.warn(`[llm] ${provider} failed - retrying`, {\n          attempt,\n          error: error.message,\n          delayMs,\n        });\n      },\n    }\n  );\n}\n\nfunction getErrorStatus(error: unknown): number | undefined {\n  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n\n  const status = (error as { status?: unknown }).status;\n  if (typeof status === 'number') {\n    return status;\n  }\n\n  const message = (error as { message?: unknown }).message;\n  if (typeof message === 'string') {\n    const match = message.match(/status\\s+(\\d{3})/i);\n    if (match) {\n      return Number(match[1]);\n    }\n  }\n\n  return undefined;\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if an error should trigger provider fallback (5xx).\n */\nexport function shouldFallback(error: unknown): boolean {\n  const status = getErrorStatus(error);\n  return typeof status === 'number' && status >= 500 && status <= 599;\n}\n","numLines":185,"startLine":1,"totalLines":185}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a86b3d36-348d-415c-add3-88a2d20876c5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Fast/cheap providers retry more; pricier or\n30\t// slower ones retry less to bound latency and spend before falling back.\n31\tfunction retriesFor(provider: LLMProvider): number {\n32\t  switch (provider) {\n33\t    case 'anthropic': return 2;\n34\t    case 'cerebras': return 0;\n35\t    case 'grok': return 1;\n36\t    case 'openai': return 1;\n37\t    case 'gemini': return 2;\n38\t    case 'mistral': return 2;\n39\t    case 'groq': return 3;\n40\t  }\n41\t  // Exhaustiveness guard: if a new LLMProvider is added to the union without a\n42\t  // case above, TypeScript flags this assignment.\n43\t  const _exhaustive: never = provider;\n44\t  throw new Error(`Unhandled LLM provider: ${String(_exhaustive)}`);\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\tfunction rawResponseLength(raw: unknown): number | undefined {\n48\t  if (typeof raw === 'string') {\n49\t    return raw.length;\n50\t  }\n51\t\n52\t  try {\n53\t    return JSON.stringify(raw).length;\n54\t  } catch {\n55\t    return undefined;\n56\t  }\n57\t}\n58\t\n59\texport async function postProcessRoute(c: Context) {\n60\t  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n61\t  const startTime = performance.now();\n62\t  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n63\t\n64\t  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n65\t    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_rejected', { reason: 'ip_blocked' });\n66\t    return errorResponse(403, 'Access denied', 'Your IP has been temporarily blocked due to abuse');\n67\t  }\n68\t  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.ip_check_done');\n69\t\n70\t  const contentType = c.req.header('Content-Type') || '';\n71\t  if (!contentType.includes('application/json')) {\n72\t    return invalidContentTypeResponse('application/json', contentType);\n73\t  }\n74\t\n75\t  let body: PostProcessBody;\n76\t  try {\n77\t    body = await c.req.json();\n78\t  } catch {\n79\t    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid JSON', 'Request body must be valid JSON');\n80\t  }\n81\t\n82\t  const text = typeof body.text === 'string' ? correctedText = text;\n225\t        } else {\n226\t          logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_incomplete', {\n227\t            provider: alternateProvider,\n228\t            state: retryEvaluation.state,\n229\t            reason: retryEvaluation.failure === 'empty_cleaned_text' ? 'empty_cleaned_text' : retryEvaluation.reason,\n230\t            fallbackToRaw: true,\n231\t          });\n232\t          correctedText = text;\n233\t        }\n234\t      } catch (retryError) {\n235\t        logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_leakage_retry_fail', {\n236\t          provider: alternateProvider,\n237\t          error: retryError instanceof Error ? retryError.message : String(retryError),\n238\t          fallbackToRaw: true,\n239\t        });\n240\t        correctedText = text;\n241\t      }\n242\t    } else {\n243\t      // output_limit, incomplete_response, or empty_cleaned_text: keep the\n244\t      // raw transcript without spending on an alternate-provider retry.\n245\t      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_incomplete', {\n246\t        provider: providerUsed,\n247\t        state: evaluation.state,\n248\t        reason: evaluation.failure === 'empty_cleaned_text' ?
