'../middleware/auth';\nimport { deductCredits, validateCredits } from '../middleware/credits';\nimport { logEvent } from '../lib/logging';\nimport { evaluateCompletionResponse } from '../lib/llm-completion';\n\nconst MAX_TEXT_LENGTH = 100000;\nconst ESTIMATED_POST_PROCESS_CREDITS = 1.0;\n\ninterface PostProcessBody {\n  text?: string;\n  prompt?: string;\n  // `account_key` is the canonical field; `license_key` is the legacy alias that\n  // installed native apps still send. Either is accepted.\n  account_key?: string;\n  license_key?: string;\n}\n\n// Per-provider primary retry count. Fast/cheap providers retry more; pricier or\n// slower ones retry less to bound latency and spend before falling back.\nfunction retriesFor(provider: LLMProvider): number {\n  switch (provider) {\n    case 'anthropic': return 2;\n    case 'cerebras': return 0;\n    case 'grok': return 1;\n    case 'openai': return 1;\n    case 'gemini': return 2;\n    case 'mistral': return 2;\n    case 'groq': return 3;\n  }\n  // Exhaustiveness guard: if a new LLMProvider is added to the union without a\n  // case above, TypeScript flags this assignment.\n  const _exhaustive: never = provider;\n  throw new Error(`Unhandled LLM provider: ${String(_exhaustive)}`);\n}\n\nfunction rawResponseLength(raw: unknown): number | undefined {\n  if (typeof raw === 'string') {\n    return raw.length;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    return JSON.stringify(raw).length;\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function postProcessRoute(c: Context) {\n  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n  const startTime = performance.now();\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_rejected', { reason: 'ip_blocked' });\n    return errorResponse(403, 'Access denied', 'Your IP has been temporarily blocked due to abuse');\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.ip_check_done');\n\n  const contentType = c.req.header('Content-Type') || '';\n  if (!contentType.includes('application/json')) {\n    return invalidContentTypeResponse('application/json', contentType);\n  }\n\n  let body: PostProcessBody;\n  try {\n    body = await c.req.json();\n  } catch {\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid JSON', 'Request body must be valid JSON');\n  }\n\n  const text = typeof body.text === 'string' ? body.text.trim() : '';\n  if (!text) {\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Missing field', 'Request body must include \"text\" field');\n  }\n  if (text.length > MAX_TEXT_LENGTH) {\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Text too long', `Text must be ${MAX_TEXT_LENGTH} characters or less`, {\n      max_length: MAX_TEXT_LENGTH,\n      actual_length: text.length,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const prompt = typeof body.prompt === 'string' ? body.prompt.trim() : '';\n  if (!prompt) {\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Missing field', 'Request body must include \"prompt\" field');\n  }\n\n  const provider = extractLLMProvider(c.req.raw);\n  const model = resolveLLMModel(provider, c.req.raw);\n\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_start', {\n    flyRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n    provider,\n    model,\n    inputChars: text.length,\n    promptChars: prompt.length,\n  });\n\n  const authResult = await validateAuth({\n    licenseKey: body.account_key || body.license_key,\n  });\n  if (!authResult.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_rejected', { reason: 'auth_failed' });\n    return authResult.response;\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.auth_done');\n\n  const creditCheck = await validateCredits(authResult.value, ESTIMATED_POST_PROCESS_CREDITS, clientIP);\n  if (!creditCheck.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_rejected', { reason: 'insufficient_credits' });\n    return creditCheck.response;\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.credits_done');\n\n  let providerUsed: LLMProvider = provider;\n  // The model actually served, tracked alongside providerUsed. Starts as the\n  // resolved request model; every fallback/leakage retry routes to the new\n  // provider's default, so we update it in lockstep.\n  let modelUsed: string = model;\n\n  const userContent = buildTranscriptUserContent(text);\n  const payload = buildCorrectionRequest(prompt, userContent);\n\n  let llmResponse: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof callWithRetry>>;\n\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_attempt_start', { provider, attempt: 1 });\n\n  try {\n    const primaryRetries = retriesFor(provider);\n    llmResponse = await callWithRetry(provider, payload, requestId, primaryRetries, model);\n  } catch (error) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_attempt_fail', {\n      provider,\n      attempt: 1,\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n\n    if (shouldFallback(error)) {\n      providerUsed = fallbackProviderFor(provider);\n      modelUsed = defaultModelFor(providerUsed);\n      const fallbackRetries = retriesFor(providerUsed);\n\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_fallback_start', { requestedProvider: provider, provider: providerUsed });\n\n      try {\n        llmResponse = await callWithRetry(providerUsed, payload, requestId, fallbackRetries, modelUsed);\n      } catch (fallbackError) {\n        logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_fail', {\n          reason: 'llm_fallback_failed',\n          provider: providerUsed,\n          error: fallbackError instanceof Error ? fallbackError.message : String(fallbackError),\n        });\n        return errorResponse(500, 'Post-processing failed', fallbackError instanceof Error ? fallbackError.message : String(fallbackError), { requestId });\n      }\n    } else {\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_fail', {\n        reason: 'llm_failed_no_fallback',\n        provider,\n        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n      });\n      return errorResponse(500, 'Post-processing failed', error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error), { requestId });\n    }\n  }\n\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_attempt_done', {\n    provider: providerUsed,\n    outputChars: rawResponseLength(llmResponse.raw),\n    costUsd: llmResponse.costUsd,\n  });\n\n  let correctedText: string;\n  let costUsd = llmResponse.costUsd;\n\n  try {\n    const evaluation = evaluateCompletionResponse(llmResponse.raw, text);\n\n    if (evaluation.accepted) {\n      correctedText = evaluation.text;\n    } else if (evaluation.failure === 'prompt_leakage') {\n      // Recomputed only for the outputChars metric below — evaluateCompletionResponse\n      // already extracted this successfully, so this call cannot throw.\n      const leakedText = stripCleanMarkers(extractCorrectedText(llmResponse.raw));\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.prompt_leakage_detected', {\n        provider: providerUsed,\n        inputChars: text.length,\n        outputChars: leakedText.length,\n      });\n\n      const alternateProvider: LLMProvider = fallbackProviderFor(providerUsed);\n      const alternateRetries = retriesFor(alternateProvider);\n\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_leakage_retry_start', { requestedProvider: providerUsed, provider: alternateProvider });\n\n      try {\n        const alternateModel = defaultModelFor(alternateProvider);\n        const retryResponse = await callWithRetry(alternateProvider, payload, requestId, alternateRetries, alternateModel);\n\n        // Bill the retry the moment it succeeds, before any evaluation step\n        // below can throw — otherwise a successful (and billed-by-the-provider)\n        // retry's cost would be silently dropped if extraction failed.\n        providerUsed = alternateProvider;\n        modelUsed = alternateModel;\n        costUsd += retryResponse.costUsd;\n\n        const retryEvaluation = evaluateCompletionResponse(retryResponse.raw, text);\n\n        if (retryEvaluation.accepted) {\n          correctedText = retryEvaluation.text;\n        } else if (retryEvaluation.failure === 'prompt_leakage') {\n          logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.prompt_leakage_persisted', {\n            provider: alternateProvider,\n            fallbackToRaw: true,\n          });\n          correctedText = text;\n        } else {\n          logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_incomplete', {\n            provider: alternateProvider,\n            state: retryEvaluation.state,\n            reason: retryEvaluation.failure === 'empty_cleaned_text' ? 'empty_cleaned_text' : retryEvaluation.reason,\n            fallbackToRaw: true,\n          });\n          correctedText = text;\n        }\n      } catch (retryError) {\n        logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_leakage_retry_fail', {\n          provider: alternateProvider,\n          error: retryError instanceof Error ? retryError.message : String(retryError),\n          fallbackToRaw: true,\n        });\n        correctedText = text;\n      }\n    } else {\n      // output_limit, incomplete_response, or empty_cleaned_text: keep the\n      // raw transcript without spending on an alternate-provider retry.\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.llm_incomplete', {\n        provider: providerUsed,\n        state: evaluation.state,\n        reason: evaluation.failure === 'empty_cleaned_text' ? 'empty_cleaned_text' : evaluation.reason,\n        fallbackToRaw: true,\n      });\n      correctedText = text;\n    }\n  } catch (extractError) {\n    // The LLM call already succeeded (and cost us money) — bill the user\n    // even though we can't return usable text.\n    deductCredits(\n      authResult.value,\n      costUsd,\n      {\n        post_processing_cost_usd: costUsd,\n        input_length: text.length,\n        output_length: 0,\n        endpoint: '/post-process',\n        llm_provider: providerUsed,\n      },\n      clientIP\n    ).catch(console.error);\n\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_fail', {\n      reason: 'extract_failed',\n      provider: providerUsed,\n      costUsd,\n      error: extractError instanceof Error ? extractError.message : String(extractError),\n    });\n    return errorResponse(500, 'Post-processing failed', extractError instanceof Error ? extractError.message : String(extractError), { requestId });\n  }\n\n  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n  deductCredits(\n    authResult.value,\n    costUsd,\n    {\n      post_processing_cost_usd: costUsd,\n      input_length: text.length,\n      output_length: correctedText.length,\n      endpoint: '/post-process',\n      llm_provider: providerUsed,\n    },\n    clientIP\n  ).catch(console.error);\n\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'post_process.request_done', {\n    finalProvider: servedLLMName(providerUsed, modelUsed),\n    inputChars: text.length,\n    outputChars: correctedText.length,\n    costUsd,\n    creditsUsed,\n    hadLeakage: correctedText === text && text.length > 0,\n  });\n\n  const response = {\n    corrected: correctedText,\n    cost: {\n      usd: costUsd,\n      credits: creditsUsed,\n    },\n  };\n\n  c.header('X-Request-ID', requestId);\n  c.header('X-LLM-Provider', servedLLMName(providerUsed, modelUsed));\n  c.header('X-Total-Cost-Usd', formatUsd(costUsd));\n  c.header('X-Credits-Used', creditsUsed.toFixed(1));\n\n  return c.json(response);\n}\n","numLines":317,"startLine":1,"totalLines":317}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"47335d90-9bf1-49e4-8823-41c302babf5f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Missing termination metadata — no response object, no stop_reason,\n16\t * no finish_reason — is `unspecified` rather than a rejection: some\n17\t * custom/self-hosted servers omit it entirely, and callers proceed to text\n18\t * handling for `unspecified` the same as they do for `complete`.\n19\t */\n20\texport function getLLMCompletionStatus(raw: unknown): LLMCompletionStatus {\n21\t  if (!isRecord(raw)) {\n22\t    return { state: 'unspecified', reason: 'missing_response_object' };\n23\t  }\n24\t\n25\t  const anthropicReason = raw['stop_reason'];\n26\t  if (typeof anthropicReason === 'string') {\n27\t    if (anthropicReason === 'end_turn' || anthropicReason === 'stop_sequence') {\n28\t      return { state: 'complete', reason: anthropicReason };\n29\t    }\n30\t    if (anthropicReason === 'max_tokens') {\n31\t      return { state: 'output_limit', reason: anthropicReason };\n32\t    }\n33\t    return { state: 'incomplete', reason: anthropicReason };\n34\t  }\n35\t\n36\t  const choices = raw['choices'];\n37\t  const firstChoice = Array.isArray(choices) ? choices[0] : undefined;\n38\t  const finishReason = isRecord(firstChoice) ? firstChoice['finish_reason'] : undefined;\n39\t\n40\t  if (finishReason === 'stop') {\n41\t    return { state: 'complete', reason: finishReason };\n42\t  }\n43\t  if (finishReason === 'length') {\n44\t    return { state: 'output_limit', reason: finishReason };\n45\t  }\n46\t  if (typeof finishReason === 'string') {\n47\t    return { state: 'incomplete', reason: finishReason };\n48\t  }\n49\t\n50\t  return { state: 'unspecified', reason: 'missing_finish_reason' };\n51\t}\n52\t\n53\texport type CompletionFailure = 'none' | 'output_limit' | 'incomplete_response' | 'prompt_leakage' | 'empty_cleaned_text';\n54\t\n55\t/**\n56\t * Composed completion policy — the TS analog of the Rust core's\n57\t * `evaluate_completion`. 'output_limit' : 'incomplete_response';\n71\t    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure, state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n72\t  }\n73\t\n74\t  const cleaned = stripCleanMarkers(extractCorrectedText(raw));\n75\t\n76\t  if (containsPromptLeakage(cleaned)) {\n77\t    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure: 'prompt_leakage', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n78\t  }\n79\t\n80\t  if (cleaned.length === 0) {\n81\t    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure: 'empty_cleaned_text', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n82\t  }\n83\t\n84\t  return { accepted: true, text: cleaned, failure: 'none', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n85\t}\n86\t"}]},"uuid":"b5202153-5753-49cd-b60e-09f6c2f5a216","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:31.999Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/llm-completion.ts","content":"import { isRecord } from './utils';\nimport { containsPromptLeakage, extractCorrectedText, stripCleanMarkers } from './text-processing';\n\nexport type LLMCompletionStatus =\n  | { state: 'complete'; reason: string }\n  | { state: 'output_limit'; reason: string }\n  | { state: 'incomplete'; reason: string }\n  | { state: 'unspecified'; reason: string };\n\n/**\n * Normalize terminal reasons from Anthropic Messages and OpenAI-compatible\n * Chat Completions responses. Recognized terminal reasons are classified as\n * complete, output-limited, or incomplete, and only a recognized\n * non-terminal reason (e.g. `length`, `content_filter`, `refusal`) fails\n * closed. Missing termination metadata — no response object, no stop_reason,\n * no finish_reason — is `unspecified` rather than a rejection: some\n * custom/self-hosted servers omit it entirely, and callers proceed to text\n * handling for `unspecified` the same as they do for `complete`.\n */\nexport function getLLMCompletionStatus(raw: unknown): LLMCompletionStatus {\n  if (!isRecord(raw)) {\n    return { state: 'unspecified', reason: 'missing_response_object' };\n  }\n\n  const anthropicReason = raw['stop_reason'];\n  if (typeof anthropicReason === 'string') {\n    if (anthropicReason === 'end_turn' || anthropicReason === 'stop_sequence') {\n      return { state: 'complete', reason: anthropicReason };\n    }\n    if (anthropicReason === 'max_tokens') {\n      return { state: 'output_limit', reason: anthropicReason };\n    }\n    return { state: 'incomplete', reason: anthropicReason };\n  }\n\n  const choices = raw['choices'];\n  const firstChoice = Array.isArray(choices) ? choices[0] : undefined;\n  const finishReason = isRecord(firstChoice) ? firstChoice['finish_reason'] : undefined;\n\n  if (finishReason === 'stop') {\n    return { state: 'complete', reason: finishReason };\n  }\n  if (finishReason === 'length') {\n    return { state: 'output_limit', reason: finishReason };\n  }\n  if (typeof finishReason === 'string') {\n    return { state: 'incomplete', reason: finishReason };\n  }\n\n  return { state: 'unspecified', reason: 'missing_finish_reason' };\n}\n\nexport type CompletionFailure = 'none' | 'output_limit' | 'incomplete_response' | 'prompt_leakage' | 'empty_cleaned_text';\n\n/**\n * Composed completion policy — the TS analog of the Rust core's\n * `evaluate_completion`. Gates on completion state, extracts and strips\n * markers, then rejects on prompt leakage or an empty result. A throw from\n * `extractCorrectedText` (a response with no text field anywhere) is\n * genuinely malformed and propagates to the caller rather than being\n * absorbed here.\n */\nexport function evaluateCompletionResponse(\n  raw: unknown,\n  original: string\n): { accepted: boolean; text: string; failure: CompletionFailure; state: LLMCompletionStatus['state']; reason: string } {\n  const status = getLLMCompletionStatus(raw);\n\n  if (status.state !== 'complete' && status.state !== 'unspecified') {\n    const failure: CompletionFailure = status.state === 'output_limit' ? 'output_limit' : 'incomplete_response';\n    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure, state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n  }\n\n  const cleaned = stripCleanMarkers(extractCorrectedText(raw));\n\n  if (containsPromptLeakage(cleaned)) {\n    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure: 'prompt_leakage', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n  }\n\n  if (cleaned.length === 0) {\n    return { accepted: false, text: original, failure: 'empty_cleaned_text', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n  }\n\n  return { accepted: true, text: cleaned, failure: 'none', state: status.state, reason: status.reason };\n}\n","numLines":86,"startLine":1,"totalLines":86}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4d42f7e7-74f1-474f-84c0-814aed02a34a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

CREDIT VALIDATION + DEDUCTION\n// Handles preflight credit checks and post-usage deduction\n\nimport type { AuthContext } from './auth';\nimport { BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE, CREDITS_PER_MINUTE, DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { roundToTenth, roundUpToTenth } from '../lib/utils';\nimport { creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { insufficientCreditsResponse } from '../lib/responses';\nimport { cacheLicense } from '../lib/redis';\n\n// Minimum encoded bitrate expected from clients for conservative credit estimation.\nconst MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS = 10;\n\nexport type CreditsResult =\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\nexport interface CreditEstimateOptions {\n  costEstimators?: Array<(durationSeconds: number) => number>;\n}\n\nexport function estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes: number): number {\n  const estimatedMinutes = sizeBytes / BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE;\n  return Math.max(MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS, estimatedMinutes * 60);\n}\n\nexport function estimateCreditsFromSize(sizeBytes: number, options: CreditEstimateOptions = {}): number {\n  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n\n  if (options.costEstimators?.length) {\n    const maxEstimatedCost = Math.max(\n      ...options.costEstimators.map((estimateCost) => estimateCost(estimatedSeconds))\n    );\n    return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(maxEstimatedCost));\n  }\n\n  const estimatedCredits = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * CREDITS_PER_MINUTE;\n  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(estimatedCredits));\n}\n\nexport async function validateCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  estimatedCredits: number,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<CreditsResult> {\n  const balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\n  if (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n    return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n  }\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nasync function recordLicenseUsage(\n  licenseKey: string,\n  creditsUsed: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n): Promise<void> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  try {\n=== deduct fn ===\n56:  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n69:        metadata,\n121:  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n124:  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n135:  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n144:  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"a3c07b68-89a1-4869-8b38-06d1c2339fa6","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:54.790Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"// CREDIT VALIDATION + DEDUCTION\n// * POST /api/license/credits\n *\n * Deduct credits from a license (record usage).\n */\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { license_key, amount, metadata } = body;\n\n    if (!license_key || typeof license_key !== \"string\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"license_key is required\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const amountError = validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount);\n\n    if (amountError) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const license = await findAccountByKey(license_key.trim());\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"License key not found\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    let newCredits: number;\n\n    try {\n      // Atomic SQL decrement (floored at 0) — concurrent POSTs cannot\n      // double-spend via a read-then-write race.\n      newCredits = await deductCreditBalance(license.userId, amount);\n    } catch (updateError) {\n      console.error(\"Credit deduction failed:\", updateError);\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"Failed to deduct credits. {"parentUuid":"b9f92bb2-d9b7-4089-8e92-756f2444cd51","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a1f765d637aca55e2","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UfDrYGeH598FtRRmirkMeG","type":"tool_result","content":" * PCM isn't produced by any client here, so WAV is the only container we can\n * cheaply confirm sync accepts). Non-WAV inputs (MP3, M4A, etc. — the common\n * case for macOS recordings and compressed imports) are rejected by the sync\n * endpoint before falling back to async, wasting a round-trip up to the sync\n * timeout on the most common input format — so sync eligibility gates on this\n * instead of attempting and eating the rejection. Mirrors the Rust core's\n * `assemblyai::sync_flow::build_sync_request`, which applies the same gate. */\nfunction isWavContentType(contentType: string): boolean {\n  return contentType.toLowerCase().includes('wav');\n}\n// The billable model is whatever AssemblyAI actually RAN (reported in the\n// completed transcript), which may differ from the requested model because\n// `speech_models` is a priority list that falls back universal-3-pro →\n// universal-2 for unsupported languages.
